### [1] [Not All Relations Rotate Alike: Transformation-Aware Decoupling for Viewpoint-Robust 3D Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27412)
*Jingjun Sun,Chaowei Wang,Zhirui Liu,Jiaxu Tian,Ming Yang,Yaoxing Wang,Shan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有3D场景图生成（3DSGG）模型在偏航视角变换下谓词预测不一致的问题，本文提出变换感知解耦（TAD）框架，根据谓词的变换行为将关系推理解耦为视角稳定分支和视角变化分支，分别学习稳定不变的线索和随观测框架变化的方向线索，最终实现视角鲁棒的3DSGG。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前3DSGG模型在智能体因偏航旋转而改变视角时，关系预测无法遵循预期的变换行为：方向性谓词（如左、前、右、后）应随观测框架变换，而接触、支撑、语义类谓词（如站立在、附着在）应保持稳定。现有模型存在这种谓词级别的变换异质性不匹配问题。

Method: 提出TAD（Transformation-Aware Decoupling）框架，将关系推理解耦为两个分支：一个学习跨视角稳定的线索，另一个学习随观测框架变化的方向线索。两个分支合并后用于标准多标签谓词预测。通过变换特定描述子和分组感知辅助监督，促使两个分支捕获互补的关系线索。

Result: 在3DSSG数据集上的大量实验表明，TAD在不使用训练时旋转增强的情况下，在偏航视角变化下达到了最先进的鲁棒性，同时在标准基准测试中保持了竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 本文验证了根据谓词变换行为解耦关系推理能有效提升3DSGG在视角变化下的鲁棒性，为具身智能中空间理解的视角适应问题提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: 3D Scene Graph Generation (3DSGG) represents 3D scenes as structured object-relation-object graphs, providing a compact relational abstraction for spatial understanding. In embodied intelligence settings, the same 3D scene may be observed by agents from viewpoints that differ by yaw rotations. However, current 3DSGG models often fail to produce relation predictions that follow the expected transformation behavior under such viewpoint shifts. This behavior reveals an empirical mismatch related to predicate-level transformation heterogeneity: directional predicates such as left, front, right, and behind should transform with the observation frame, whereas most contact, support, and semantic predicates such as standing on and attached to should remain stable. To reduce this mismatch, we propose Transformation-Aware Decoupling (TAD), a viewpoint-robust 3DSGG framework that decouples relation reasoning according to predicate transformation behavior and is supported by viewpoint-stable object representations. TAD decomposes relation reasoning into two parts: one learns cues that should stay stable across viewpoints, while the other learns directional cues that should change with the observation frame. The two parts are merged for standard multi-label predicate prediction. Transformation-specific descriptors and group-aware auxiliary supervision encourage the two branches to capture complementary relation cues. Extensive experiments on 3DSSG show that TAD achieves state-of-the-art robustness under yaw viewpoint changes without training-time rotation augmentation, while maintaining competitive performance under the standard benchmark. The project page is available at https://tad-predicate.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 3D场景图生成（3DSGG）将3D场景表示为结构化的物体-关系-物体图，为空间理解提供了紧凑的关系抽象。在具身智能场景中，同一3D场景可能被智能体从偏航旋转不同的视角进行观测。然而，当前的3DSGG模型在这种视角偏移下往往无法产生遵循预期变换行为的关系预测。这种行为揭示了与谓词级别变换异质性相关的经验性不匹配：方向性谓词（如左、前、右、后）应随观测框架变换，而大多数接触、支撑和语义谓词（如站立在、附着在）应保持稳定。为减少这种不匹配，我们提出了变换感知解耦（TAD），一种视角鲁棒的3DSGG框架，它根据谓词变换行为解耦关系推理，并由视角稳定的物体表示支撑。TAD将关系推理分解为两部分：一部分学习跨视角应保持稳定的线索，另一部分学习应随观测框架变化的方向线索。两部分合并后用于标准多标签谓词预测。变换特定描述子和分组感知辅助监督促使两个分支捕获互补的关系线索。在3DSSG上的大量实验表明，TAD在不使用训练时旋转增强的情况下，在偏航视角变化下达到了最先进的鲁棒性，同时在标准基准测试中保持了竞争力的性能。项目页面可见 https://tad-predicate.github.io/。

</details>


### [2] [SemCityLoc: Aerial 6DoF Localization Using Semantic 3D City Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27444)
*Jingfeng Mao,Xuyang Chen,Qilin Zhang,Oussema Dhaouadi,Guangming Wang,Brian Sheil,Daniel Cremers,Yan Xia,Olaf Wysocki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemCityLoc提出一种基于语义-几何对齐的空中6DoF定位方法，通过将基础模型导出的视觉先验与标准化LoD城市模型进行结构化表面配准，无需GNSS或辐射度量重建，在挑战性城市环境中将召回率提升36%，位置误差从9.89m降至2.62m。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统空中6DoF定位要么依赖高精度GNSS信号，要么需要辐射度量丰富的3D重建，这限制了可扩展性和机载部署能力。现有基于地图的方法主要匹配稀疏轮廓或密集纹理，在重复和遮挡的城市环境中区分度不足。

Method: 将空中位姿估计重新定义为结构化表面配准问题，将基础模型（如SAM等）导出的语义视觉先验与标准化LoD（Level of Detail，LoD1-LoD3）3D城市模型中的语义表面进行对齐，同时结合单目深度信息，实现轻量级的语义几何匹配。

Result: 在自建的SemCityLockeD基准数据集上，相比现有地图方法，召回率提升最高36%，平均位置误差从9.89m降至2.62m。实验表明语义结构化几何约束足以提供高精度空中定位。

Conclusion: 语义结构化几何无需辐射度量场景重建即可为空中定位提供足够且可扩展的约束，是一种更简洁、可部署性更强的空中定位范式。

Original Abstract: Aerial 6DoF localization typically relies on precise GNSS signals or radiometrically rich 3D reconstructions, limiting scalability and on-board deployment. We propose SemCityLoc, a semantic-geometric alignment system that reframes aerial pose estimation as structured surface registration between foundation-model-derived visual priors and standardized LoD-compliant 3D city models. Instead of matching sparse contours or dense texture, our method aligns semantic surfaces and monocular depth with lightweight semantic 3D building models, increasing pose discriminability in repetitive and occluded urban environments. To enable accurate evaluation, we introduce SemCityLockeD, the first real-world benchmark combining centimeter-accurate UAV poses with standardized LoD1--LoD3 semantic city models and challenging low-altitude imagery. Experiments demonstrate substantial improvements over existing map-based approaches, improving recall by up to 36% and reducing mean positional error from 9.89m to 2.62m in challenging urban canyons. Our results indicate that semantically structured geometry provides sufficient and scalable constraints for high-precision aerial localization without radiometric scene reconstructions. The code and data are available at https://albertchen98.github.io/SemCityLoc.

中文摘要翻译: 空中6DoF定位通常依赖高精度GNSS信号或辐射度量丰富的3D重建，这限制了可扩展性和机载部署能力。本文提出SemCityLoc——一种语义-几何对齐系统，将空中位姿估计重新定义为基于基础模型导出的视觉先验与标准化LoD兼容3D城市模型之间的结构化表面配准。该方法不匹配稀疏轮廓或密集纹理，而是将语义表面和单目深度与轻量级语义3D建筑模型对齐，在重复和遮挡的城市环境中提升位姿区分度。为实现精确评估，我们引入SemCityLockeD——首个结合厘米级精度无人机位姿、标准化LoD1-LoD3语义城市模型以及挑战性低空影像的真实世界基准。实验表明，与现有基于地图的方法相比，该方法在挑战性城市峡谷中召回率提升36%，平均位置误差从9.89m降至2.62m。结果表明，语义结构化几何无需辐射度量场景重建即可为高精度空中定位提供充分且可扩展的约束。代码和数据已在https://albertchen98.github.io/SemCityLoc公开。

</details>


### [3] [Fine-tuning a multimodal large language model for clinician-grade autism behavioral scoring from short home videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27484)
*Mohammadmahdi Honarmand,Parnian Azizian,Aaron Kline,Kae Nurge,Zerin Nasrin Tumpa,Saimourya Surabhi,Kaitlyn Dunlap,Yang Qian,Ali Kargarandehkordi,Sameer Neupane,Peter Washington,Dennis P. Wall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对Gemini 2.5 Pro多模态大语言模型进行低秩适配微调，利用400个临床医师评分的家庭视频和30个行为特征，在自闭症谱系障碍(ASD)诊断任务上，将行为特征与临床医师间信度提升40%，零样本ASD诊断F1提升53%，分类器辅助诊断准确率达77%，AUC达86%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: ASD影响1/31的美国儿童，但诊断中位年龄超过4岁，亟需利用易获取的观察数据（如家庭视频）实现更早的量化诊断，从而及时开展早期干预。

Method: 在400个临床医师评分的家庭视频上，采用低秩适配(LoRA)微调Gemini 2.5 Pro模型，仅训练30个经验证的行为特征，并在99名保留儿童（49名ASD，50名神经典型）上进行评估。

Result: (1) 行为特征的临床医师间信度（加权Cohen's kappa）提升40%（p<0.001），28个可评估特征中有27个改善；(2) 零样本ASD诊断F1提升53%（p<0.001），达到或超过临床医师水平；(3) 分类器辅助管道使用微调LLM提取的行为特征达到77%准确率（95% CI: 68-85%）和86% AUC（95% CI: 78-92%）。

Conclusion: 微调的多模态大语言模型可作为可扩展的行为特征提取器，用于自闭症评估和诊断。

Original Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) affects 1 in 31 US children, yet median age at diagnosis exceeds four years. Artificial intelligence pipelines that provide quantified diagnosis using easy to access observational data (e.g., home videos) could help with earlier diagnosis, and timely delivery of early treatments. We fine-tuned Gemini 2.5 Pro on 400 clinician-rated home videos with low-rank adaptation, training only on 30 behavioral features previously validated to produce reliable predictions when passed to various ML models. On 99 held-out children (49 ASD, 50 neurotypical), inter-rater reliability with clinicians (per-feature weighted Cohen's kappa) improved by 40% (p<0.001), with 27 of 28 evaluable features improving. As an emergent zero-shot capability, direct ASD diagnosis F1 improved by 53% (p<0.001), matching or exceeding clinician outcomes. Classifier-assisted pipelines using fine-tuned LLM-derived behavioral features matched clinician-scored inputs across all tested pathways and achieved 77% accuracy (95% CI: 68-85%) and an AUC of 86% (95% CI: 78-92%). Fine-tuned multimodal LLMs can serve as scalable behavioral feature extractors for use in autism assessment and diagnosis.

中文摘要翻译: 自闭症谱系障碍(ASD)影响每31名美国儿童中就有1名，但诊断中位年龄超过四岁。利用易于获取的观察数据（如家庭视频）提供量化诊断的人工智能管线有助于实现更早的诊断以及及时提供早期治疗。我们在400个临床医师评分的家庭视频上使用低秩适配微调了Gemini 2.5 Pro，仅训练30个行为特征，这些特征此前已验证可向各种机器学习模型传递可靠预测。在99名保留儿童（49名ASD，50名神经典型）上，与临床医师之间的信度（每特征加权Cohen's kappa）提升了40%（p<0.001），28个可评估特征中有27个得到改善。作为涌现的零样本能力，直接ASD诊断F1提升了53%（p<0.001），达到或超过临床医师的结果。使用微调LLM提取的行为特征的分类器辅助管线在所有测试路径上均匹配临床医师评分的输入，并达到77%的准确率（95% CI: 68-85%）和86%的AUC（95% CI: 78-92%）。微调后的多模态大语言模型可作为可扩展的行为特征提取器，用于自闭症评估和诊断。

</details>


### [4] [SelectAnyTree: A Promptable Instance Segmentation Model for 3D Forest LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27491)
*Trung Thanh Nguyen,Daniel Lusk,Kilian Gerberding,Janusch Vajna-Jehle,Tuan-Anh Vu,Duc Viet Le,Tu Vo,Phi Le Nguyen,Yasutomo Kawanishi,Takahiro Komamizu,Ichiro Ide,Julian Frey,Teja Kattenborn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SelectAnyTree是一种基于提示的3D森林点云实例分割模型，仅需少量点击即可分割任意单棵树，通过点击到查询的提示编码器和CHM引导的首个提示机制，在多个森林区域上取得了优于现有方法的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 森林LiDAR点云的自动化实例分割面临标注稀缺的瓶颈：单公顷森林包含数百万点云和数百棵重叠树冠，人工标注成本高且易错，现有自动预分割方法缺乏交互式或AI辅助的精细化能力。

Method: 提出SelectAnyTree模型，包含两个核心组件：(1) Click-to-query提示编码器，将每次点击转换为包含3D位置、正/负极性及局部骨干特征的内容查询；(2) CHM引导的首个提示，利用冠层高度模型自动提供树梢作为几何和生态学引导的初始提示。提示查询通过状态空间查询解码器解码为单个树掩码，以线性时间复杂度高效捕捉大规模森林场景的长程上下文。

Result: 在七个不同森林区域和独立测试集上，SelectAnyTree单次点击达到78.2 IoU，比最强基线高出24.8个百分点；以最少点击达到所有精度目标，且参数量和推理时间远少于先前的提示模型。

Conclusion: SelectAnyTree验证了提示式分割范式在3D森林点云实例分割中的有效性，能够以极少的交互代价实现高精度单棵树分割，并展现出跨域泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Automated instance segmentation of forest LiDAR point clouds is increasingly critical as forest monitoring moves toward scalable, detailed, 3D measurement. Yet, progress is constrained by label scarcity for tree instances; a single hectare can hold millions of points and hundreds of overlapping, complex crowns, making manual annotation from scratch with raw data laborious and error-prone. Annotations are often corrected from automatic pre-segmentations, but remain costly as these provide no interactive or AI-assisted refinement. Inspired by the promptable paradigm of foundation segmentation models, we propose SelectAnyTree, a promptable instance segmentation model that delineates any individual tree in a 3D forest point cloud from a few clicks. It introduces two key components: Click-to-query prompt encoder and Canopy Height Model (CHM)-guided first prompt. The former turns each click into a single content query, encoding its 3D position and positive/negative polarity together with a pooled local backbone feature. The latter provides treetops as a geometry- and ecologically guided first prompt without any user input. The resulting prompt query is then decoded into one tree mask by a state-space query decoder to efficiently capture long-range context in large-scale forest scenes with linear-time complexity. We evaluate SelectAnyTree in interactive and instance-level settings across seven diverse forest regions and an independent held-out test dataset, demonstrating strong generalization beyond the training domains. It segments a target tree to 78.2 Intersection over Union (IoU) from a single click, 24.8 points above the strongest promptable baseline, and reaches every accuracy target with the fewest clicks, while using far fewer parameters and less inference time than prior promptable models. The source code is available at https://github.com/thanhhff/SelectAnyTree.

中文摘要翻译: 随着森林监测向可扩展、精细化的3D测量发展，森林LiDAR点云的自动化实例分割日益关键。然而，树实例的标注稀缺制约了进展：单公顷森林可包含数百万个点和数百个重叠、复杂的树冠，这使得从原始数据手动标注既费力又易出错。标注通常基于自动预分割结果进行校正，但由于缺乏交互式或AI辅助的精化手段，成本仍然高昂。受基础分割模型中可提示范式的启发，我们提出SelectAnyTree——一种可提示的实例分割模型，能够从几次点击中勾勒出3D森林点云中的任意单棵树。模型引入两个关键组件：点击到查询的提示编码器和冠层高度模型（CHM）引导的首个提示。前者将每次点击转化为单一内容查询，编码其3D位置和正/负极性以及池化的局部骨干特征；后者无需任何用户输入，提供树梢作为几何和生态学引导的首个提示。生成的提示查询随后由状态空间查询解码器解码为一个树掩码，以线性时间复杂度高效捕获大规模森林场景中的长程上下文。我们在七个不同森林区域和一个独立的保留测试数据集上，以交互式和实例级别两种设置评估SelectAnyTree，展示了超越训练域的强泛化能力。该模型单次点击即可达到78.2的交并比（IoU），比最强的可提示基线高出24.8个百分点，以最少点击达到所有精度目标，同时使用的参数和推理时间远少于先前的可提示模型。源代码已开源：https://github.com/thanhhff/SelectAnyTree。

</details>


### [5] [DMV-Bench: Diagnosing Long-Horizon Multimodal Agents' Visual Memory with Incidental Cue Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27499)
*Yujin Tang,Chenming Shang,Ruize Xu,Nikhil Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了DMV-Bench，首个多模态智能体视觉记忆交互式基准，以及受双编码理论启发的DualMem记忆架构，在视觉记忆任务上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有智能体记忆研究几乎完全基于文本，缺乏评估智能体在交互环境中真正需要记住视觉信息的基准。作者旨在填补多模态智能体视觉记忆评测的空白。

Method: 构建DMV-Bench基准（基于家居电商目录，含1000个产品变体，通过文本泄漏契约确保任务区分信号仅存于像素中）；提出DualMem架构，并行维护视觉编码和语言编码，基于双编码理论。

Result: DualMem在Gemini 2.5 Flash和Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，于所有链长度J∈{5,10,15,50}上均优于字幕基线和三个近期多模态智能体记忆系统；领先优势在记忆库大小和编码位置偏差控制下依然保持；非对称双编码模式下视觉端到端承载线索，语言通道仅起查询定位辅助作用。

Conclusion: 视觉记忆对多模态智能体至关重要，双编码架构能有效提升视觉记忆性能，且视觉通道应承担主要记忆负载。

Original Abstract: Research on agent memory has matured rapidly, but almost entirely on the text side: few existing benchmarks ask, in an interactive environment, when an agent genuinely needs to remember what it saw rather than what it could write down. We introduce DMV-Bench (Code: https://github.com/yyyujintang/DMV-Bench), the first interactive benchmark for multimodal-agent visual memory. DMV-Bench is built on a controlled home-furnishing e-commerce catalogue of 1,000 product variants in which a text-leakage contract keeps the discriminative signal of each task in the pixels alone. Across a chain of autonomous shopping sessions, every visited product image carries a unique, pre-rendered incidental cue, and the agent is later asked to recall a particular cued product and navigate to its URL. Inspired by dual-coding theory, we propose DualMem, a memory architecture that maintains a visual and a verbal code in parallel. On DMV-Bench, DualMem outperforms a caption baseline and three recent multimodal agent-memory systems at every chain length J in {5, 10, 15, 50} on both Gemini 2.5 Flash and Qwen2.5-VL-7B, with the lead surviving controls for memory-bank size and encoding-position bias, and an asymmetric dual-coding regime in which vision carries the cue end-to-end while the verbal channel plays a smaller query-grounding role.

中文摘要翻译: 关于智能体记忆的研究已快速成熟，但几乎完全集中在文本层面：很少有现有的基准在交互环境中询问智能体何时真正需要记住它所看到的而非它能写下的内容。我们提出了DMV-Bench（代码：https://github.com/yyyujintang/DMV-Bench），这是首个面向多模态智能体视觉记忆的交互式基准。DMV-Bench构建于一个受控的家居电商产品目录之上，包含1000个产品变体，通过文本泄漏契约保证每个任务的区分信号仅存于像素中。在一系列自主购物会话中，每次访问的产品图像都携带一个独特的、预渲染的附带线索，智能体随后需要被要求回忆某个特定线索对应的产品并导航至其URL。受双编码理论的启发，我们提出了DualMem，一种并行维护视觉编码和语言编码的记忆架构。在DMV-Bench上，DualMem在Gemini 2.5 Flash和Qwen2.5-VL-7B两个模型上，于所有链长度J∈{5,10,15,50}上均优于字幕基线和三个近期的多模态智能体记忆系统；其领先优势在控制记忆库大小和编码位置偏差后依然存在。我们发现了一种非对称双编码机制：视觉通道端到端地承载线索，而语言通道仅扮演较小的查询定位辅助角色。

</details>


### [6] [An Embedded Real-Time License Plate Recognition System for Complex Traffic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27772)
*Anuki Pasqual,Dulan Lokugeegana,Manimohan Thiriloganathan,Nuthya Rathnayake,Kithsiri Samarasinghe,Udaya S. K. P. Miriya Thanthrige*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向发展中国家的嵌入式实时车牌识别系统，采用轻量级CNN进行车牌检测和字符识别，通过量化与FPGA加速在Xilinx Kria KV260平台上实现11.5 FPS的实时推理，并发布了包含复杂交通场景的斯里兰卡车牌数据集SL-LPR。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 发展中国家交通场景复杂、车辆种类多样，且传统车牌识别系统部署成本高，难以在资源受限的嵌入式平台上实现实时性能。现有系统多针对发达国家的规整场景，缺乏对非结构化交通环境的适配。

Method: 采用两阶段流水线：首先使用轻量级CNN对多车辆图像进行车牌检测，然后对检测到的车牌进行字符识别；使用Brevitas库进行低位宽量化，并利用FINN框架在FPGA上实现模型加速，最终部署于Xilinx Kria KV260嵌入式平台。

Result: 在SL-LPR数据集上，车牌检测模型达到93.6% mAP，字符识别模型达到87.88%准确率；在多个公开数据集上与更大模型相比具有竞争力；端到端系统在KV260平台上达到11.5 FPS的实时性能。

Conclusion: 该系统在嵌入式设备上实现了有效的实时车牌识别，即使在复杂交通场景下也能良好运行，验证了轻量级模型结合量化与FPGA加速在低成本部署场景下的可行性。

Original Abstract: Vehicle license plate recognition is an integral component of intelligent transportation systems. In this work, we present an embedded real-time license plate recognition system customized for developing countries. We address the challenge of handling complex, unstructured traffic scenes with diverse vehicle types while implementing the system on an embedded platform for low-cost deployment. Our method consists of license plate detection on a multi-vehicle image, followed by character recognition on the detected license plates. Both steps use lightweight convolutional neural networks to balance accuracy and efficiency. We also introduce the SL-LPR dataset of Sri Lankan road images, which contains a variety of vehicle types and traffic conditions typically seen in developing countries. On this dataset, the license plate detection and character recognition models achieved 93.6% mAP and 87.88% accuracy, respectively, and were competitive against larger models on several public datasets. To achieve real-time performance in a resource-constrained embedded environment, we applied low-bitwidth quantization using the Brevitas library and implemented FPGA acceleration for the models using the FINN framework. The end-to-end system can operate at 11.5~FPS when implemented on the Xilinx Kria KV260 platform. These results demonstrate that our system is effective for real-time license plate recognition on an embedded device, even in complex traffic scenarios. The SL-LPR dataset is available for research use at: https://github.com/sl-lpr-uom/SL-LPR.git.

中文摘要翻译: 车辆牌照识别是智能交通系统的重要组成部分。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种专为发展中国家定制的嵌入式实时车牌识别系统。我们解决了处理包含多种车辆类型的复杂非结构化交通场景的挑战，同时在嵌入式平台上实现该系统以降低部署成本。我们的方法包括对多车辆图像进行车牌检测，然后对检测到的车牌进行字符识别。两个步骤均使用轻量级卷积神经网络以平衡准确性和效率。我们还介绍了斯里兰卡道路图像数据集SL-LPR，该数据集包含了发展中国家常见的多种车辆类型和交通状况。在该数据集上，车牌检测和字符识别模型分别达到了93.6%的mAP和87.88%的准确率，并在多个公开数据集上与更大规模的模型具有竞争力。为了在资源受限的嵌入式环境中实现实时性能，我们使用Brevitas库应用了低位宽量化，并使用FINN框架为模型实现了FPGA加速。该端到端系统在Xilinx Kria KV260平台上可实现11.5 FPS的运行速度。这些结果表明，即使在复杂的交通场景下，我们的系统在嵌入式设备上也能实现有效的实时车牌识别。SL-LPR数据集可在https://github.com/sl-lpr-uom/SL-LPR.git获取，供研究使用。

</details>


### [7] [Aloe-Vision: Robust Vision-Language Models for Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27500)
*Jaume Guasch-Martí,Enrique Lopez-Cuena,Martín Suárez-Fernández,Jordi Bayarri-Planas,Anna Arias-Duart,Dario Garcia-Gasulla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Aloe-Vision系列医疗大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM），包括大规模质量过滤数据集Aloe-Vision-Data、两个规模的模型（7B和72B）以及CareQA-Vision基准测试，证明了高质量训练混合数据能提升医疗性能而不损害通用能力，同时揭示了当前LVLM在临床场景下的鲁棒性不足问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医疗领域大型视觉-语言模型的发展受到高质量多模态数据稀缺、安全关键场景下的鲁棒性担忧以及评估基准狭窄且可能被污染的限制，因此亟需构建完全开放、可复现的系统，使所有组件均可被检查、评估和改进。

Method: 构建大规模质量过滤多模态数据集Aloe-Vision-Data（融合医疗与通用域的多模态及纯文本数据），在此基础上训练Aloe-Vision系列模型（7B和72B规模），并引入CareQA-Vision基准（基于西班牙MIR和EIR住院医师考试构建的低污染视觉问答基准）。

Result: 高质量训练混合数据能产生平衡的LVLM，在医疗任务上显著优于基线模型且不损害通用能力，性能与最先进替代方案相当；但当前LVLM仍易受对抗性和误导性输入攻击，在临床场景中存在可靠性挑战。

Conclusion: 开放性、高质量的数据与模型系统对推动医疗LVLM发展至关重要，但模型在临床关键场景中的鲁棒性仍需进一步提升。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) specialized in healthcare are emerging as a promising research direction due to their potential impact in clinical and biomedical applications. However, progress is constrained by the scarcity of high-quality medical multimodal data, concerns about robustness in safety-critical settings, and the narrow and potentially contaminated evaluation benchmarks that limit reliable assessment. To address these issues, the field requires state-of-the-art solutions to be fully open and reproducible systems in which all components can be inspected, evaluated, and improved. This work introduces Aloe-Vision-Data, a large-scale, quality-filtered mixture which integrates both medical and general domains across multimodal and text-only sources, designed for direct use in model fine-tuning. Building on this dataset, we train the Aloe-Vision family of medical LVLMs, openly released with full weights, training recipes and data, in two scales (7B and 72B). Through comprehensive benchmarking, we demonstrate that high quality training mixtures produce balanced LVLMs which yield significant gains over the baseline models without compromising general capabilities, achieving competitive performance with respect to state-of-the-art alternatives. To support reliable evaluation, we introduce CareQA-Vision, a carefully curated vision benchmark derived from MIR and EIR exams, the residency entrance exams for medical and nursing specialists in Spain, offering novel vision questions with low likelihood of contamination. Finally, we show that current LVLMs remain vulnerable to adversarial and misleading inputs, underscoring reliability challenges in clinical contexts.

中文摘要翻译: 专门用于医疗保健的大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）因其在临床和生物医学应用中的潜在影响而成为一个有前景的研究方向。然而，进展受到高质量医疗多模态数据稀缺、安全关键场景下的鲁棒性担忧，以及狭窄且可能被污染的评估基准限制。为解决这些问题，该领域需要完全开放且可复现的最先进系统，使其所有组件都可供检查、评估和改进。本文介绍了Aloe-Vision-Data，一个大规模、质量过滤的混合数据集，整合了医疗和通用领域中的多模态及纯文本来源，设计用于直接的模型微调。基于该数据集，我们训练了Aloe-Vision系列的医疗LVLM，以两种规模（7B和72B）公开释放全部权重、训练配方和数据。通过全面的基准测试，我们证明高质量训练混合数据能产生平衡的LVLM，在不损害通用能力的情况下显著优于基线模型，并与最先进替代方案达到竞争性性能。为支持可靠评估，我们引入了CareQA-Vision，一个精心策划的视觉基准，源自西班牙医学和护理专科住院医师考试（MIR和EIR），提供了新颖的、被污染可能性低的视觉问题。最后，我们表明当前的LVLM仍然容易受到对抗性和误导性输入的攻击，突显了临床环境中的可靠性挑战。

</details>


### [8] [ReWorld: Learning Better Representations for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27504)
*Tianze Xia,Lijun Zhou,Kaixin Xiong,Jingfeng Yao,Yu Zhu,Zhenxin Zhu,Bing Wang,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Wenyu Liu,Haiyang Sun,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReWorld是首个专为自动驾驶世界动作模型（WAM）设计的表示学习框架，通过对中间表示进行未来预测监督、跨模态对齐和困难负样本边界判别学习，显著提升了视频生成质量、闭环规划性能和训练收敛速度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有WAM研究主要关注模型架构设计，而如何让WAM高效学习更好的世界表示以支持规划决策这一问题尚未被充分探索。标准训练方式仅监督生成和规划模块的输出端，导致携带世界知识的中间表示只能作为输出拟合的副产品被间接塑造。

Method: ReWorld将中间表示作为直接优化目标，沿三个互补维度进行塑造：1）在生成模块（Video DiT）上对未来进行预测性监督；2）在规划模块（Action DiT）上通过跨模态对齐将中间表示与视频世界表示对齐；3）通过困难负样本监督使表示在安全关键边界附近具有判别性。

Result: 在nuScenes和NAVSIM数据集上，ReWorld将微调后的视频生成FVD从81.3降至61.9（提升23.9%），闭环PDMS从89.1提升至90.4且无需RL或后处理，从头训练的收敛速度加速约2倍。

Conclusion: 通过直接优化中间表示而非仅监督输出端，可以显著提升WAM在视频生成质量、规划性能和训练效率方面的表现，为自动驾驶世界模型提供了一种有效的表示学习范式。

Original Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) model future environment evolution under action conditioning, offering a scalable paradigm for autonomous driving. However, existing approaches focus largely on model architecture design, and how a WAM can efficiently learn better world representations for planning remains underexplored. To address this gap, we propose ReWorld, the first representation learning framework specifically designed for autonomous-driving world action models. In WAMs, standard training supervises only the output ends of the generation and planning modules, leaving the intermediate representations that carry world knowledge to be shaped only indirectly, as byproducts of fitting these outputs. The core idea of ReWorld is to treat intermediate representations as direct targets of optimization, shaping them along three complementary dimensions. On the Video DiT responsible for generation, we impose future-predictive supervision on its intermediate representations. On the Action DiT responsible for planning, we first align its intermediate representations cross-modally with the video world representation, then further shape them to be discriminative around safety-critical boundaries via hard-negative supervision. In addition, we systematically analyze the effectiveness of existing representation learning methods in video generation world models, and discuss why their performance is limited on this task. Experiments on nuScenes and NAVSIM show that ReWorld improves fine-tuned video generation by 23.9% in FVD (81.3 to 61.9), raises closed-loop PDMS from 89.1 to 90.4 without any post-training such as RL or post-processing, and accelerates from-scratch convergence by approximately 2x.

中文摘要翻译: 世界动作模型（WAMs）在动作条件作用下建模未来环境演化，为自动驾驶提供了一种可扩展的范式。然而，现有方法主要关注模型架构设计，而WAM如何高效学习更好的世界表示以支持规划决策这一问题仍未得到充分探索。为填补这一空白，我们提出ReWorld，这是首个专门为自动驾驶世界动作模型设计的表示学习框架。在WAM中，标准训练仅监督生成和规划模块的输出端，导致承载世界知识的中间表示只能作为拟合这些输出的副产品而被间接塑造。ReWorld的核心思想是将中间表示作为直接优化目标，沿三个互补维度对其进行塑造。在负责生成的Video DiT上，我们对其中间表示施加未来预测监督。在负责规划的Action DiT上，我们首先将其中间表示与视频世界表示进行跨模态对齐，进而通过困难负样本监督使其在安全关键边界附近具有判别性。此外，我们系统分析了现有表示学习方法在视频生成世界模型中的有效性，并讨论了它们在该任务上性能有限的原因。在nuScenes和NAVSIM上的实验表明，ReWorld将微调后的视频生成FVD从81.3降至61.9（提升23.9%），将闭环PDMS从89.1提升至90.4，且无需任何强化学习或后处理等事后训练，同时将从头训练的收敛速度加速约2倍。

</details>


### [9] [TruEye: Fine-Grained Detection of AI-Generated Human Subjects in Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27505)
*Jay Barot,Dan Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TruEye提出了一种掩码条件双流Transformer模型，用于细粒度检测和定位AI生成或篡改的人脸与场景，能够区分五种合成类别，速度比LLM方法快100倍以上，并在多个数据集上取得SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器存在三大问题：一是过度拟合训练时见过的生成器，泛化能力差；二是将检测视为黑盒二分类，缺乏可解释性；三是依赖昂贵的大语言模型（LLM）进行解释，推理成本高。

Method: 提出TruEye模型，核心是一个掩码条件双流Transformer架构：将人体和场景令牌分离并保持patch级空间对应；每个流内部进行专门推理，通过区域门控交叉注意力确保主体与背景之间的语义一致性；利用令牌级监督和全局组合分类实现可解释预测，无需依赖LLM。

Result: 在6个公开数据集和新构建的FineSyn数据集上，TruEye在检测准确率、推理速度和泛化能力上均超越现有SOTA检测器，速度比基于LLM的竞品快100倍以上。

Conclusion: TruEye通过双流Transformer架构实现了对AI生成/篡改图像的细粒度、可解释且高效的检测，在准确率、速度和泛化性方面全面超越现有方法，为社交媒体上的合成内容检测提供了实用解决方案。

Original Abstract: AI generated images are proliferating across the Internet. While some are used for entertainment, others are weaponized for fraud and social engineering attacks on social media users. Existing detectors overfit to generators seen during training, treat detection as opaque binary classification, or rely on costly Large Language Models (LLMs) to explain their outputs. In this paper, we present TruEye, a novel model for fine grained detection and localization of AI manipulated or AI generated humans and scenes. Unlike conventional detectors that assign a single authenticity label, TruEye is the first to distinguish among five compositional categories of synthetic content, including the most challenging case in which a real human is composited into a real scene where they were never physically present. At its core is a mask conditioned dual stream transformer that separates human and scene tokens while preserving patch level spatial correspondence. Specialized reasoning within each stream and region gated cross attention enforce semantic coherence between subject and background, while token level supervision and global compositional classification yield robust, interpretable predictions without invoking an LLM. By restricting intra stream attention to semantically coherent tokens, TruEye also runs over $100\times$ faster than LLM based competitors. Experiments on 6 datasets and our newly curated FineSyn dataset, show that TruEye surpasses state of the art detectors with higher accuracy, faster inference, and stronger generalization to unseen AI generated or manipulated images.

中文摘要翻译: AI生成的图像正在互联网上大量扩散。虽然其中一些用于娱乐目的，但另一些则被武器化用于对社交媒体用户实施欺诈和社会工程攻击。现有的检测器过度拟合训练阶段见过的生成器，将检测视为不透明的二分类任务，或依赖昂贵的大语言模型（LLM）来解释其输出。在本文中，我们提出TruEye，一种用于细粒度检测和定位AI操纵或AI生成的人体与场景的新型模型。与分配单一真伪标签的传统检测器不同，TruEye是首个能够区分五种合成内容组合类别的模型，包括最具挑战性的情况：真实人物被合成到其从未实际出现的真实场景中。其核心是一个掩码条件双流Transformer，该架构将人体令牌和场景令牌分离，同时保持patch级的空间对应关系。每个流内部的专门推理以及区域门控交叉注意力确保了主体与背景之间的语义一致性，而令牌级监督和全局组合分类则在不调用LLM的情况下产生鲁棒且可解释的预测。通过将流内注意力限制在语义连贯的令牌上，TruEye的运行速度比基于LLM的竞品快100倍以上。在6个数据集以及我们新构建的FineSyn数据集上的实验表明，TruEye超越了现有的最先进检测器，具有更高的准确率、更快的推理速度，以及对未见过的AI生成或操纵图像更强的泛化能力。

</details>


### [10] [Structured-Li-GS: Structured 3D Gaussians Splatting with LiDAR Incorporation and Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27509)
*Huaiyuan Weng,Huibin Li,Chul Min Yeum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Structured-Li-GS，一种结合LiDAR-惯性-视觉SLAM的轻量级3D高斯泼溅框架，通过利用精确、密集的彩色点云来锚定高斯原语并初始化其椭球参数，在无需高斯致密化的情况下实现高质量3D重建，且模型大小适中、结果达到尺度一致的高保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅方法通常需要大量高斯原语和繁重的致密化过程，导致模型体积庞大且重建质量受限；同时缺乏对LiDAR等几何先验信息的有效利用，难以在保持轻量级的同时实现高精度、尺度一致的3D重建。

Method: 提出Structured-Li-GS框架，核心包括：(1) 利用LiDAR-惯性-视觉SLAM生成精确、密集的彩色点云；(2) 使用子采样点云锚定高斯原语，并从局部表面几何初始化椭球参数；(3) 设计综合训练损失函数，包含光度损失、展平损失、偏移损失、深度损失和法向损失，由密集点云引导，无需高斯致密化即可实现精确重建。

Result: 在基准数据集和自建真实世界数据集上的实验表明，Structured-Li-GS在比现有方法使用更少高斯原语的情况下，在重建质量上超越了当前最先进的方法，实现了尺度一致的高保真重建结果。

Conclusion: 本文证明，通过将LiDAR几何先验信息有效融入3D高斯泼溅框架，可以在无需高斯致密化的情况下、以更轻量的模型实现更高质量的重建，为3DGS的实际应用提供了高效且精确的解决方案。

Original Abstract: In this study, we develop a Structured framework for Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with LiDAR integration (Structured-Li-GS). It is a lightweight Gaussian Splatting pipeline that leverages LiDAR-inertial-visual SLAM. Structured-Li-GS achieves high-quality 3D reconstructions with fewer Gaussians by training on accurate, dense, colorized point clouds. Gaussian primitives are anchored using sub-sampled point clouds, and their ellipsoidal parameters are initialized from local surface geometry. Our training strategy integrates a comprehensive set of loss terms, including photometric, flattening, offset, depth, and normal losses, guided by the dense point cloud, enabling accurate reconstruction without Gaussian densification. This approach produces up-to-scale, high-fidelity results with a moderate model size. For experimental validation, we develop a custom hardware-synchronized LiDAR-camera handheld scanner. Experiments on both benchmark datasets and our real-world in-house dataset demonstrate that Structured-Li-GS surpasses state-of-the-art methods while using fewer Gaussians.

中文摘要翻译: 在本研究中，我们开发了一种结合LiDAR的结构化高斯泼溅框架（Structured-Li-GS）。这是一个轻量级的高斯泼溅流水线，利用LiDAR-惯性-视觉SLAM技术。Structured-Li-Gs通过训练精确、密集的彩色点云，用更少的高斯原语实现高质量的三维重建。高斯原语使用子采样点云进行锚定，其椭球参数根据局部表面几何进行初始化。我们的训练策略整合了一套全面的损失函数，包括光度损失、展平损失、偏移损失、深度损失和法向损失，并由密集点云引导，从而在无需高斯致密化的情况下实现精确重建。该方法以适中的模型大小产生尺度一致的高保真结果。为进行实验验证，我们开发了一款自定义硬件同步的LiDAR-相机手持扫描仪。在基准数据集和我们自建的室内真实数据集上的实验表明，Structured-Li-GS在使用更少高斯原语的情况下超越了当前最先进的方法。

</details>


### [11] [Tessellating The Earth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27514)
*Daniel Cher,Hamza Iqbal,Eric Xing,Brian Wei,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TTE(Tessellating the Earth)，一种基于可学习球面Voronoi划分的地理位置编码器，通过将表示容量聚焦于高判别性区域，并引入全局语义令牌来共享相似环境的地理语义信息，在多项地理空间分类与回归任务中达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有地理位置编码器将坐标投影到固定基函数上（如球谐函数），均匀分配表示容量，导致开放海洋和发展中城市被同等对待，无法将有限的计算资源聚焦于信息更丰富的区域。

Method: 1）构建可学习的球面Voronoi划分，每个Voronoi站点携带独立嵌入并在训练过程中向高判别性区域迁移；2）引入全局语义令牌（global semantic tokens），通过一组共享的可学习概念令牌，从卫星图像中蒸馏语义知识到位置编码器可引用的紧凑词汇表中，使地理上相距遥远但环境相似的站点共享语义信息。整个过程完全可微、端到端可训练。

Result: TTE在一系列地理空间分类与回归任务中达到当前最优水平；在iNaturalist-2018细粒度物种分类任务中作为地理先验使用时取得了最强结果。

Conclusion: TTE通过可学习的非均匀空间划分和全局语义共享机制，有效提升了地理位置编码器的表示效率和泛化能力，验证了将表示容量按需分配的有效性。

Original Abstract: Geolocation encoders, which map geographic coordinates to learned representations, are emerging as an effective means of capturing visual and non-visual characteristics from a latitude-longitude pair alone. However, existing approaches project coordinates onto fixed bases (e.g., spherical harmonics), allocating representational capacity uniformly and devoting equal resources to the open ocean and to a developing city. We introduce Tessellating the Earth (TTE), a location encoder built from learnable Spherical Voronoi partitions that concentrates representational capacity where it is needed in a fully differentiable, end-to-end manner. Each Voronoi site carries its own embedding and migrates during training toward discriminative areas. To bridge the gap between local spatial structure and global semantic understanding, we introduce \emph{global semantic tokens}: a set of shared learnable concept tokens that distill semantic knowledge from the satellite imagery into a compact vocabulary the location encoder can reference at inference, enabling geographically distant sites covering similar environments to share semantics. TTE sets a new state of the art for location encoders across a suite of geospatial classification and regression tasks, and achieves the strongest results when used as a geographic prior for fine-grained species classification on iNaturalist-2018. Code, and weights are available at https://github.com/mvrl/TTE.

中文摘要翻译: 地理位置编码器将地理坐标映射到学习到的表示，已成为仅从经纬度对捕捉视觉和非视觉特征的有效手段。然而，现有方法将坐标投影到固定基函数（如球谐函数）上，均等地分配表示容量，对开阔海洋和发展中城市投入相同资源。本文提出TTE（Tessellating the Earth），一种基于可学习球面Voronoi划分的位置编码器，能以完全可微、端到端的方式将表示容量聚焦于需要的地方。每个Voronoi站点携带自身嵌入，并在训练过程中向高判别性区域迁移。为弥合局部空间结构与全局语义理解之间的鸿沟，我们引入了全局语义令牌（global semantic tokens）：一组共享的可学习概念令牌，从卫星图像中蒸馏语义知识到一个紧凑的词汇表中，位置编码器可在推理时引用该词汇表，使地理上相距遥远但覆盖相似环境的站点能够共享语义。TTE在一系列地理空间分类与回归任务中达到地理位置编码器的最新最优水平，并在iNaturalist-2018细粒度物种分类中作为地理先验使用时取得了最强结果。代码和模型权重见https://github.com/mvrl/TTE。

</details>


### [12] [Large Language Model Teaches Visual Students: Cross-Modality Transfer of Fine-Grained Conceptual Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27527)
*Thomas Shih-Chao Liang,Zhuoran Yu,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LaViD框架，通过让纯语言LLM生成多项选择题来构建类别语义签名，将语言知识蒸馏到纯视觉学生模型中，无需多模态数据配对，在多个细粒度基准上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LLM通过大规模文本预训练获得了广泛的概念知识，但将其用于监督其他模态（尤其是纯视觉）模型的潜力尚未被充分探索。现有知识蒸馏方法往往依赖多模态数据或视觉-语言模型，而LaViD旨在利用纯语言教师的语义知识来指导纯视觉学生模型。

Method: LaViD框架包含三个步骤：(1) 向纯语言LLM提示生成关于视觉类别间语义区分的多项选择题；(2) 将每个类别映射到这些MCQ上的软标签分布，形成丰富的概念签名；(3) 在训练视觉学生模型时，通过辅助蒸馏损失使其输出分布与这些概念签名对齐。

Result: 在多个细粒度基准上，LaViD始终优于从视觉-语言模型蒸馏的MaKD等方法；与DKD、MLKD等SOTA视觉蒸馏方法相比具有竞争力或更优性能；在Waterbirds数据集上显著提升了最差组精度，增强了模型对虚假相关性的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 纯语言模型的概念知识可以有效地蒸馏到纯视觉模型中，无需依赖配对的图像-文本数据，且能带来更强的泛化性能和鲁棒性，为跨模态知识迁移提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) possess broad conceptual knowledge acquired through large-scale text pretraining, yet their potential to supervise models in other modalities remains underexplored. In this work, we propose LaViD--Language-to-Visual Knowledge Distillation--a simple and effective framework for transferring high-level semantic knowledge from a language-only teacher to a vision-only student model. Instead of relying on paired multimodal data, LaViD elicits conceptual signals from an LLM by prompting it to generate multiple-choice questions (MCQs) that probe semantic distinctions between visual classes. Each class is mapped to a soft label distribution over these MCQs, forming a rich conceptual signature that guides the student through an auxiliary distillation loss. Notably, despite using a language-only teacher without access to image data, LaViD consistently outperforms recent methods like MaKD that distill from vision-language models across multiple fine-grained benchmarks. It also achieves competitive or superior performance compared to state-of-the-art visual distillation methods such as DKD and MLKD, with further gains when combined with logit standardization. On the Waterbirds dataset, LaViD substantially improves worst-group accuracy, demonstrating enhanced robustness to spurious correlations with distillation. Code is available at https://github.com/lliangthomas/lavid.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）通过大规模文本预训练获得了广泛的概念知识，但其在监督其他模态模型方面的潜力尚未被充分探索。本文提出LaViD——语言到视觉知识蒸馏（Language-to-Visual Knowledge Distillation）——一个简单而有效的框架，用于将高层语义知识从纯语言教师模型迁移到纯视觉学生模型。LaViD不依赖配对的跨模态数据，而是通过提示LLM生成探测视觉类别间语义区分的多项选择题（MCQ）来 elicit 概念信号。每个类别被映射到这些MCQ上的一个软标签分布，形成丰富的概念签名，通过辅助蒸馏损失指导学生模型。值得注意的是，尽管使用的是无法访问图像数据的纯语言教师，LaViD在多个细粒度基准上始终优于MaKD等从视觉-语言模型蒸馏的最新方法。与DKD和MLKD等最先进的视觉蒸馏方法相比，LaViD也取得了具有竞争力或更优的性能，并且结合logit标准化后能获得进一步提升。在Waterbirds数据集上，LaViD显著提高了最差组精度，证明了蒸馏对虚假相关性的鲁棒性增强。代码已开源：https://github.com/lliangthomas/lavid。

</details>


### [13] [MemoBench: Benchmarking World Modeling in Dynamically Changing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27537)
*Haoyu Chen,Kaichen Zhou,Hang Hua,Kaile Zhang,Jingwen Qian,Wufei Ma,Haonan Chen,Chunjiang Liu,Yizhou Zhao,Xiaoyuan Wang,Weiyue Li,Alan Yuille,Paul Pu Liang,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MemoBench 基准，评估视频生成模型在物体消失后重新出现时记忆一致性的能力，填补现有基准仅评估物体在视野内或静态场景下的空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型评估基准大多仅评估目标在视野内时的记忆一致性，少数评估物体出视野的基准也只针对静态场景（遮挡期间无变化）。缺乏对动态环境中物体消失-重现过程的评估。

Method: 构建 MemoBench 基准，采用 disappear-and-reappear 范式：目标物体经历物理过程后消失，再出现时必须恢复更新后的状态。收集360个真实片段（含合成和真实场景），设计结合自动指标和 VQA 的评估套件，覆盖四个诊断支柱。

Result: 评估了八个最新模型，揭示了在消失-重现范式下记忆一致性的关键洞见和开放挑战。

Conclusion: 现有模型在动态环境中的物体消失-重现记忆一致性方面仍存在显著不足，MemoBench 为该问题提供了系统化的诊断基准。

Original Abstract: Video generation models aspire to simulate dynamic environments, and several benchmarks now evaluate memory consistency across frames. However, most assess consistency only while the target remains in view, and the few that force objects out of view evaluate static scenes where nothing changes during occlusion. To bridge this gap, we introduce MemoBench, a diagnostic benchmark built around the disappear-and-reappear paradigm in dynamically changing environments: a target object undergoes a physical process, disappears from view, and must be correctly recovered in its updated state upon reappearance. We curate 360 ground-truth clips spanning synthetic and real-world scenes, and design an evaluation suite combining automated metrics with VQA-based assessment across four diagnostic pillars. Evaluation of eight state-of-the-art models reveals key insights and open challenges regarding memory consistency under the disappear-and-reappear paradigm.

中文摘要翻译: 视频生成模型旨在模拟动态环境，已有多个基准评估跨帧的记忆一致性。然而，大多数基准仅在目标保持在视野内时评估一致性，少数迫使物体出视野的基准则评估静态场景——遮挡期间没有任何变化。为填补这一空白，我们提出了 MemoBench，一个围绕动态环境中"消失-重现"范式构建的诊断基准：目标物体经历一个物理过程后从视野中消失，并在重新出现时必须正确恢复其更新后的状态。我们整理了360个涵盖合成场景和真实场景的真实片段，并设计了一个结合自动化指标与基于 VQA 的评估套件，覆盖四个诊断支柱。对八个最先进模型的评估揭示了在"消失-重现"范式下关于记忆一致性的关键洞见和开放挑战。

</details>


### [14] [Beyond MoCap: Scaling Motion Tokenizers with Synthetic Human Motion for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27547)
*Yiwen Yan,Wanning He,Yu-Wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过大规模合成人体运动数据扩展运动表示空间的框架，结合改进的VQ-VAE分词器，显著提升运动生成的覆盖性、组合性和泛化能力，实验表明数据瓶颈比模型架构更为关键。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有运动捕捉数据集多样性不足，主要包含常见重复动作，无法覆盖复杂人体运动的长尾分布，导致学到的运动词汇受限，模型在稀有、组合和高度动态运动上泛化能力差。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：(1) 构建大规模合成人体运动数据生成流水线，产生超出已有数据集分布的多样化、物理合理的运动序列；(2) 重新设计VQ-VAE分词器，使其适应扩展后的运动空间，联合缩放训练分布和离散码本，以捕获更丰富的运动基元。

Result: 合成运动训练显著提升了运动词汇的覆盖率和组合性；在文本生成运动和运动延续任务上持续取得收益；该方法与MotionGPT等现有框架完全兼容。

Conclusion: 运动生成的主要瓶颈在于所学运动表示的支持范围有限，而非模型架构本身；通过同步缩放合成运动数据与表示学习，为实现更具表现力、可控性和泛化能力的人体运动合成提供了可行路径。

Original Abstract: Human motion generation models are fundamentally constrained by the limited diversity of motion capture datasets, which predominantly contain common, repetitive actions and fail to cover the long tail of complex human movements, resulting in a restricted motion vocabulary in learned latent representations and poor generalization to rare, compositional, and highly dynamic motions. In this work, we propose a framework for expanding the motion representation space by leveraging large-scale synthetic human motion, introducing a data generation pipeline that produces diverse, physically plausible motion sequences beyond the distribution of existing datasets and integrating it with a redesigned VQ-VAE tokenizer that adapts to this expanded motion space. Unlike conventional tokenizers trained on narrow data distributions, our approach jointly scales both the training distribution and the discrete codebook, enabling the model to capture a significantly richer set of motion primitives. We demonstrate that training with synthetic motion substantially improves the coverage and compositionality of the learned motion vocabulary, leading to consistent gains across motion generation tasks such as text-to-motion and motion continuation, while remaining fully compatible with existing frameworks including MotionGPT. Our results suggest that the primary bottleneck lies in the limited support of the learned motion representation, rather than model architecture alone. Scaling synthetic motion in tandem with representation learning offers a principled path toward more expressive, controllable, and generalizable human motion synthesis.

中文摘要翻译: 人体运动生成模型从根本上受限于运动捕捉数据集的有限多样性，这些数据集主要包含常见的重复性动作，无法覆盖复杂人体运动的长尾分布，导致学到的潜在表示中运动词汇受限，并且在稀有、组合和高度动态的运动上泛化能力较差。在这项工作中，我们提出了一个通过利用大规模合成人体运动来扩展运动表示空间的框架，引入了一个数据生成流水线，该流水线能够生成超出已有数据集分布的多样化、物理合理的运动序列，并将其与一个适应于该扩展运动空间的重新设计的VQ-VAE分词器集成。与在狭窄数据分布上训练的传统分词器不同，我们的方法联合缩放训练分布和离散码本，使模型能够捕获显著更丰富的运动基元集。我们证明了使用合成运动进行训练显著改善了所学运动词汇的覆盖率和组合性，在文本生成运动和运动延续等运动生成任务上持续取得收益，同时与包括MotionGPT在内的现有框架完全兼容。我们的结果表明，主要瓶颈在于所学运动表示的支持范围有限，而非模型架构本身。同步缩放合成运动与表示学习为实现更具表现力、可控性和泛化能力的人体运动合成提供了一条原则性路径。

</details>


### [15] [Understanding Cross-Rig Generalization in Automotive Perception: a Multi-Rig Benchmark and Rig Variation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27554)
*Tim Alexander Bader,Tim Dieter Eberhardt,Maximilian Dillitzer,Wilhelm Stork*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动驾驶多视角感知系统在不同相机配置（传感器支架）间的泛化问题，构建了包含14种系统化相机支架的基准测试集，并提出了两种基于标定的描述符（Rig Variance和Rig Contrastive Distance）来量化支架差异与性能迁移之间的关系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际自动驾驶车队中相机的位置、朝向、视场角和数量存在显著差异，而现有感知系统通常基于固定传感器支架开发和评估，导致跨支架领域鸿沟（cross-rig domain gap），即只有几何观测过程发生变化。作者希望在该受控条件下系统研究这一影响。

Method: 1) 构建Plentiful CARLA Camera Rigs基准测试集，在CARLA模拟器中渲染相同驾驶场景下的14种系统化设计的相机支架；2) 提出两种基于标定的描述符：Rig Variance（捕获支架内部多样性）和Rig Contrastive Distance（衡量支架间的几何差异）；3) 在基准上分析代表性多视图感知架构的跨支架迁移行为。

Result: 几何支架差异会导致显著的性能变化；几何支架差异与跨支架相对性能变化强相关；Rig Contrastive Distance可作为预测传感器支架间迁移难度的可靠代理指标。

Conclusion: 几何支架差异是导致跨支架性能变化的关键因素，Rig Contrastive Distance能够有效量化并预测不同传感器配置间的迁移难度，为自动驾驶感知系统的跨平台部署提供了理论依据和实用工具。

Original Abstract: Camera-based perception systems for autonomous driving are typically developed and evaluated using fixed sensor rigs, while real-world vehicle fleets exhibit substantial variation in camera placement, orientation, field of view, and camera count. This mismatch introduces a cross-rig domain gap in which only the geometric observation process changes. To study this effect under controlled conditions, we introduce Plentiful CARLA Camera Rigs, a benchmark that renders identical driving scenes under 14 systematically designed camera rigs. This setup enables direct analysis of cross-rig generalization without confounding changes in scene content or appearance. Using the benchmark, we analyze cross-rig transfer behavior of representative multi-view perception architectures and observe substantial performance shifts induced by geometric rig variation. To facilitate structured analysis, we further introduce two calibration-based descriptors derived from rig metadata: Rig Variance, capturing internal rig diversity, and Rig Contrastive Distance, measuring geometric discrepancy between rigs. Our experiments show that geometric rig differences strongly correlate with relative cross-rig performance shifts and that Rig Contrastive Distance provides a reliable proxy for ranking transfer difficulty between sensor rigs.

中文摘要翻译: 用于自动驾驶的基于摄像头的感知系统通常使用固定的传感器支架进行开发和评估，而真实世界的车辆车队在相机位置、朝向、视场角和相机数量上表现出显著差异。这种不匹配引入了跨支架领域鸿沟，其中仅有几何观测过程发生变化。为了在受控条件下研究这一影响，我们引入了Plentiful CARLA Camera Rigs——一个在14种系统化设计的相机支架下渲染相同驾驶场景的基准测试集。该设置能够在不混淆场景内容或外观变化的情况下，直接分析跨支架泛化能力。利用该基准测试集，我们分析了代表性多视图感知架构的跨支架迁移行为，并观察到由几何支架变化引起的显著性能迁移。为了促进结构化分析，我们进一步引入了两种基于标定的描述符，这些描述符源自支架元数据：Rig Variance，用于捕获支架内部的多样性；以及Rig Contrastive Distance，用于衡量支架间的几何差异。我们的实验表明，几何支架差异与跨支架相对性能变化强相关，并且Rig Contrastive Distance可以作为传感器支架间迁移难度的可靠代理指标。

</details>


### [16] [Radar Guided Camera Verification for Automatic Emergency Braking Rethinking Object Detection in Radar Camera Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27556)
*Ram Charan Akula,Sivanathan Kandhasamy,Manikandan Ganesan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种雷达引导的边密度门控方法，无需训练数据和GPU即可在AEB系统中实现高效障碍物验证，将相机搜索空间减少98.7%，延迟仅0.121ms，召回率0.994，零漏刹车。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有雷达-相机融合AEB系统依赖计算密集型的目标检测器进行视觉验证，效率较低。既然雷达已定位目标，相机只需验证障碍物存在而非完整识别，因此需要一种轻量级、无需训练数据的方法。

Method: 提出雷达引导的边密度门控（Radar Scoped Edge Density Gate）方法，在雷达引导的图像感兴趣区域（ROI）内利用边缘密度进行障碍物验证，无需训练数据、模型权重或GPU加速，集成到完整的雷达-相机融合AEB线控制动系统中。

Result: 在真实车辆上72次驾驶会话、131,603帧图像中，搜索空间减少98.7%，平均处理延迟0.121ms/ROI，AUC为0.898，召回率0.994。在33个威胁场景中，完整AEB系统零漏刹车。

Conclusion: 所提方法在极低计算开销下实现了高精度的障碍物验证，无需训练数据，适合资源受限的AEB部署场景。

Original Abstract: Radar camera fusion is widely used in Automatic Emergency Braking AEB systems because radar provides reliable range and velocity measurements while cameras provide a proper visual confirmation of the objects . Most of the deployed systems perform this confirmation using computationally intensive object detectors. However, if the radar has already localized a target, the camera may only need to verify the obstacles presence rather than solving a full problem by identifying the object. Our work proposes a radar scoped edge density gate that performs obstacle verification within radar guided image regions of interest. This method requires no training data, model weights, or GPU acceleration and was integrated into a complete radar camera fusion AEB system with brake by wire actuation. Evaluated on a real instrumented vehicle across 72 driving sessions and 131,603 camera frames, the proposed approach reduced the camera search space by up to 98.7 percentage, achieved a mean processing latency of 0.121 ms per ROI, an AUC of 0.898, and a recall of 0.994. Across 33 staged threat scenarios, the complete AEB system recorded zero missed brake events.

中文摘要翻译: 雷达-相机融合被广泛用于自动紧急制动（AEB）系统，因为雷达提供可靠的测距和速度测量，而相机则提供对目标的适当视觉确认。大多数已部署的系统使用计算密集型的物体检测器来执行这种确认。然而，如果雷达已经定位了目标，相机可能只需要验证障碍物的存在，而不是通过识别物体来解决完整的问题。我们的工作提出了一种雷达引导的边密度门控方法，在雷达引导的图像感兴趣区域内执行障碍物验证。该方法无需训练数据、模型权重或GPU加速，并已集成到一个带有线控制动执行机构的完整雷达-相机融合AEB系统中。在一辆真实仪器化车辆上进行的72次驾驶会话和131,603帧相机图像的评估中，所提出的方法将相机搜索空间减少了高达98.7%，实现了每个ROI平均0.121毫秒的处理延迟、0.898的AUC和0.994的召回率。在33个预设威胁场景中，完整的AEB系统记录了零漏刹车事件。

</details>


### [17] [Perceptual 3D Simulation With Physical World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27575)
*Wanhee Lee,Klemen Kotar,Rahul Mysore Venkatesh,Jared Watrous,Daniel L. K. Yamins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P3Sim提出了一种结合学习型物理世界模型、几何条件模块和持久场景记忆的系统，能够在部分观测和不完整3D变换信号下模拟未来场景状态，泛化到新视角合成、物体操作和动态场景预测等多种任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实世界的系统必须依赖本质上不完整和不全面的感知输入与局部动作，而理想模拟器拥有完整的3D几何和动力学信息。因此，需要一种能够在部分观测和不完整3D变换信号下进行物理世界建模和场景预测的系统。

Method: P3Sim由三个交互组件构成：(1) 学习型物理世界模型，将感知解释为多模态场景变量上的概率推理，提供任意变量在条件下的分布预测；(2) 几何条件模块，提供部分3D变换信号以在推理时条件化世界模型；(3) 持久场景记忆，随时间整合预测，实现在线更新和不确定性下的一致性。

Result: 该方法在新视角合成、物体操作和动态场景预测等多种3D变换任务上展现出良好的泛化能力，验证了数据驱动灵活性与显式几何归纳偏置相结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将学习推理与显式几何结构相结合，P3Sim在灵活性与归纳偏置之间取得平衡，为通用3D场景理解和变换提供了有效的感知模拟器。

Original Abstract: Predicting how a scene will evolve after a desired 3D transformation from images is a central goal in vision, graphics, and robotics. Yet unlike ideal simulators with full access to 3D geometry and dynamics, real world systems must rely on perceptual inputs and local actions that are inherently partial and incomplete. In this work, we present P3Sim, a physical world modeling system that simulates future scene states under both partial observations and incomplete 3D transformation signals. P3Sim is composed of three interacting components: a learned physical world model, a geometric conditioning module, and a persistent scene memory. The world model interprets perception as probabilistic inference over multimodal scene variables, providing predictions of the distributions of any scene variable conditioned on any combination of others. The geometric conditioning module provides a partial 3D transform signal for conditioning the world model at inference time. The persistent scene memory integrates predictions over time, enabling online updates and consistency under uncertainty. By combining learned inference with explicit geometric structure, P3Sim balances data-driven flexibility with built-in inductive bias. This design yields a flexible perceptual simulator that generalizes across diverse 3D transformation tasks, such as novel view synthesis, object manipulation, and dynamic scene prediction, advancing toward general purpose 3D scene understanding and transformation.

中文摘要翻译: 从图像中预测场景在期望的3D变换后如何演变，是视觉、图形学和机器人学的核心目标。然而，与拥有完整3D几何和动力学信息的理想模拟器不同，现实世界的系统必须依赖本质上不完整和不全面的感知输入与局部动作。在这项工作中，我们提出了P3Sim，一个物理世界建模系统，它能够在部分观测和不完整3D变换信号下模拟未来场景状态。P3Sim由三个交互组件构成：一个学习型物理世界模型、一个几何条件模块和一个持久场景记忆。世界模型将感知解释为多模态场景变量上的概率推理，提供任意场景变量在任意其他变量条件化下的分布预测。几何条件模块提供部分3D变换信号，用于在推理时条件化世界模型。持久场景记忆随时间整合预测，实现在线更新和不确定性下的一致性。通过将学习推理与显式几何结构相结合，P3Sim在数据驱动的灵活性与内置的归纳偏置之间取得平衡。这一设计产生了一个灵活的感知模拟器，能够泛化到多种不同的3D变换任务，如新视角合成、物体操作和动态场景预测，从而推进通用3D场景理解与变换的发展。

</details>


### [18] [DeLux: Cross-Modal Local Artifact Restoration in Video Using Neuromorphic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27576)
*Bartosz Stachowiak,Dariusz Brzezinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用神经形态事件流作为结构先验的跨模态恢复范式DeLux，用于检测和修复RGB视频中的光照伪影（耀斑、眩光、闪烁和过曝），在合成和真实场景中均显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统RGB相机受光照伪影（如耀斑、眩光、闪烁、过曝）影响会导致不可逆的信息丢失，而现有方法孤立地处理每种伪影，无法恢复被复杂空间离散退化完全遮挡的结构细节。

Method: 提出DeLux——一种模块化概念验证流水线，利用神经形态事件流作为结构先验，引导RGB视频中光照伪影的针对性检测和修复，实现跨模态恢复。

Result: 在合成基准和真实汽车场景数据上，DeLux有效抑制局部伪影并恢复受损区域；平均MS-SSIM超过0.99，在真实汽车场景中伪影严重程度降低最高达88%，优于现有RGB-only基线方法和事件引导的HDR模型。

Conclusion: 跨模态恢复范式（利用事件流作为结构先验）能够有效处理RGB视频中的复杂光照伪影，为相关研究提供了新的方向，同时公开了合成工具和评估数据集以促进后续研究。

Original Abstract: Conventional RGB cameras suffer from lighting artifacts such as flare, glare, flicker, and overexposure, leading to irrecoverable information loss that necessitates computational restoration. However, existing approaches treat these problems in isolation, failing to recover structural details completely obscured by complex spatially discrete image degradations. In this paper, we propose a novel cross-modal restoration paradigm and present DeLux, a modular proof-of-concept pipeline that leverages neuromorphic event streams as a structural prior to guide the targeted detection and inpainting of lighting artifacts in RGB video. Validation on synthetic benchmarks and real-world automotive footage demonstrates that DeLux effectively suppresses local artifacts and restores affected regions. The proposed approach outperforms existing RGB-only baselines and event-guided HDR models, achieving an average MS-SSIM of over 0.99 across all artifact types and demonstrating up to an 88% reduction in artifact severity in real-world automotive footage. The synthetic artifact generation tools and curated real-world evaluation datasets are made publicly available to foster future research on cross-modal restoration.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的RGB相机容易受到诸如耀斑、眩光、闪烁和过曝等光照伪影的影响，导致不可逆的信息丢失，因而需要计算恢复。然而，现有方法孤立地处理这些问题，无法恢复被复杂空间离散图像退化完全遮挡的结构细节。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的跨模态恢复范式，并展示了DeLux——一个模块化的概念验证流水线，该流水线利用神经形态事件流作为结构先验，引导RGB视频中光照伪影的针对性检测和修复。在合成基准和真实汽车场景视频上的验证表明，DeLux能够有效抑制局部伪影并恢复受影响区域。所提方法优于现有的RGB-only基线方法和事件引导的HDR模型，在所有伪影类型上平均MS-SSIM超过0.99，并在真实汽车场景视频中展现出高达88%的伪影严重程度降低。我们公开了合成的伪影生成工具和精心整理的真实评估数据集，以促进未来在跨模态恢复方面的研究。

</details>


### [19] [Distribution-based deep multiple instance learning for tumor proportion scoring in NSCLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27579)
*Krzysztof Pysz,Artur Bartczak,Jarosław Kwiecień,Piotr Krajewski,Witold Dyrka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于零膨胀贝塔(ZIBeta)分布建模的多实例学习框架，通过端到端方式从整张病理切片预测非小细胞肺癌的肿瘤比例评分(TPS)，显著提升了预测准确性和可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TPS评分依赖繁琐的手动标注且合格专家稀缺；现有MIL方法在处理无表达（零类）图像时表现不佳，亟需一种更准确且可解释的自动化TPS预测方法。

Method: 提出两阶段端到端框架：(1) 嵌入提取与多分类网络，捕获单个病理图像块的组学特征；(2) MIL模型聚合这些嵌入，预测反映整张切片TPS概率分布的零膨胀贝塔(ZIBeta)分布参数。

Result: ZIBeta建模在预测精度上显著优于基线的线性回归和岭回归，且通过分布集中度有效反映了预期的预测准确性。

Conclusion: 基于ZIBeta分布的新型架构能够利用仅有的切片级TPS标签，在端到端训练中提升TPS预测的准确性和可解释性，为肺癌治疗规划提供更可靠的自动评估工具。

Original Abstract: Accurate assessment of tumor proportion score (TPS) in non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) is critical for treatment planning and prognosis. Key challenges include the tedious manual work required to annotate each slide, combined with the limited number of experts certified for this task. Multiple instance learning (MIL) has proven to be an effective approach for predicting TPS scores at the slide level; however, existing methods struggle with non-expressive (zero class) images. Our approach involves two models: (1) an embedding-extraction and multiclass-classification network that captures the histopathological features of individual patches, and (2) a MIL model that aggregates these embeddings to predict zero-inflated beta (ZIBeta) parameters representing the overall TPS probability distribution for the entire slide. Using only slide-level TPS scores as labels, we demonstrate how this end-to-end framework can leverage a novel distribution-based architecture to improve prediction accuracy and explainability. ZIBeta modeling significantly outperforms baseline linear and ridge regression while capturing expected accuracy through distribution concentration.

中文摘要翻译: 准确评估非小细胞肺癌(NSCLC)的肿瘤比例评分(TPS)对于治疗规划和预后判断至关重要。主要挑战包括：标注每张病理切片需要繁琐的人工操作，以及通过此项认证的专家数量有限。多实例学习(MIL)已被证明是预测切片级TPS评分的有效方法；然而，现有方法在处理无表达（零类）图像时表现不佳。我们的方法包含两个模型：(1) 一个嵌入提取与多分类网络，用于捕获单个病理图像块的组织病理学特征；(2) 一个MIL模型，聚合这些嵌入以预测零膨胀贝塔(ZIBeta)分布参数，该分布表示整张切片的整体TPS概率分布。仅使用切片级TPS评分作为标签，我们展示了这种端到端框架如何利用基于分布的新型架构来提升预测精度和可解释性。ZIBeta建模在性能上显著优于基线的线性回归和岭回归，同时通过分布集中度有效反映了预期的预测准确性。

</details>


### [20] [Beyond Points: Spherical Distributional Part Prototypes for Interpretable Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27582)
*Duarte Leão,Diogo Pereira Araújo,Catarina Barata,Carlos Santiago*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出vMFProto，一种基于von Mises-Fisher分布的原型神经网络框架，通过分布化原型表示和最优传输驱动的分配机制，在保持高解释质量的同时实现竞争性分类精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于点原型的可解释神经网络存在两个问题：1）现代视觉骨干网络工作在归一化方向嵌入空间，语义部分存在显著的类内变异，导致点原型冗余或不稳定；2）点原型无法捕捉每个语义部分的特定变异性，损害了解释质量和鲁棒性。

Method: 1）将每个类别建模为超球面上von Mises-Fisher混合分布，每个原型学习自身的浓度参数以捕捉部分特异性变异；2）采用熵正则化最优传输（OT）获得结构化的patch-to-prototype分配；3）两阶段训练：OT驱动的原型发现阶段 + 端到端精炼阶段（包含patch级蒸馏和分布感知多样性正则化）。

Result: 在CUB-200-2011、Stanford Dogs和Stanford Cars数据集上，使用冻结的DINO骨干网络，vMFProto在解释质量（一致性、稳定性和区分性）上达到最先进水平，同时保持有竞争力的分类准确率。定性结果证实vMFProto产生局部化、非冗余的部分证据。

Conclusion: 分布化原型表示（vMFProto）能够有效克服点原型在方向嵌入空间中的局限性，通过捕获类内变异和最优传输分配显著提升可解释神经网络的解释质量与鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Prototype-based neural networks aim to provide intrinsic interpretability by grounding predictions in a small set of part prototypes. However, modern vision backbones typically operate in normalized, directional embedding spaces where each semantic part exhibits substantial intra-class variability. As a result, point prototypes often become redundant or unstable, hurting both explanation quality and robustness. We propose vMFProto, a distributional part-prototype framework that models each class as a mixture of von Mises-Fisher components on the hypersphere. Each prototype learns its own concentration, capturing part-specific variability, and we use entropic optimal transport (OT) to obtain structured patch-to-prototype assignments. A two-stage training schedule performs OT-driven prototype discovery followed by end-to-end refinement with patch-level distillation and distribution-aware diversity regularization. Experiments on CUB-200-2011, Stanford Dogs, and Stanford Cars with frozen DINO backbones show that vMFProto achieves state-of-the-art explanation quality (consistency, stability, and distinctiveness) with competitive accuracy. Qualitative results confirm that vMFProto yields localized, non-redundant part evidence.

中文摘要翻译: 基于原型的神经网络旨在通过将预测锚定在一小组部分原型上来提供内在可解释性。然而，现代视觉骨干网络通常工作在归一化的方向嵌入空间中，其中每个语义部分表现出显著的类内变异性。因此，点原型往往变得冗余或不稳定，损害了解释质量和鲁棒性。本文提出vMFProto，一个分布化部分原型框架，将每个类别建模为超球面上von Mises-Fisher分量的混合。每个原型学习自身的浓度参数，捕捉部分特异性变异，并使用熵正则化最优传输（OT）获得结构化的patch到原型的分配。两阶段训练策略执行OT驱动的原型发现，随后通过patch级蒸馏和分布感知多样性正则化进行端到端精炼。在CUB-200-2011、Stanford Dogs和Stanford Cars数据集上使用冻结的DINO骨干网络的实验表明，vMFProto在解释质量（一致性、稳定性和区分性）上达到最先进水平，同时保持有竞争力的分类准确率。定性结果证实，vMFProto产生局部化、非冗余的部分证据。

</details>


### [21] [CoIn: Comprehensive 2D-3D Inpainting with Gaussian Splatting Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27584)
*Hana Kim,Minje Kim,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoIn提出了一种通过多阶段一致性流程桥接2D修复模型和3D高斯泼溅的框架，支持任意形状掩码的物体移除与插入任务，实现了双向信息流动下的最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的3D编辑方法依赖精确的多视图分割掩码，且局限于物体移除任务，无法处理更灵活的编辑需求（如物体插入）。

Method: 首先利用扩散模型生成初始修复图像（2D）；然后引入参考自适应高斯泼溅与特征注意力机制，通过自适应加权参考视图重建粗3D场景（2D→3D）；接着利用高斯泼溅参考特征扭曲为扩散过程提供几何引导，确保多视图一致性（3D→2D）；最后使用纹理增强判别器提升3D场景的光度真实感（2D→3D）。

Result: 实验表明CoIn有效利用了双向信息流，在物体移除和插入任务上均达到最优性能，且支持灵活的掩码输入。

Conclusion: 通过双向信息流动（2D↔3D）的多阶段一致性管道，CoIn成功拓展了3D场景修复的适用范围，兼顾了物体移除与物体插入任务。

Original Abstract: 3D scene inpainting is essential for reconstructing areas corrupted by occlusions or limited viewpoints. While recent methods leverage Gaussian Splatting (GS) for efficient 3D editing, they often depend on precise multi-view segmentation masks and are inherently constrained to object removal tasks. We propose CoIn, a novel framework that bridges 2D inpainting models and 3DGS through a multi-stage consistency pipeline. Our approach first generates initial inpainted images using a diffusion model, enabling the use of arbitrary-shaped masks and diverse tasks like object insertion. We then introduce Reference Adaptive GS with Feature Attention to reconstruct a coarse 3D scene by adaptively weighing towards a reference view (2D -> 3D). This 3D representation provides geometric guidance to the diffusion process via GS-based Reference Feature Warping, ensuring multi-view consistency (3D -> 2D). Finally, a Texture-Enhancing Discriminator refines the 3D scene to achieve high photometric realism (2D -> 3D). Experiments show that CoIn, effectively leveraging bidirectional information flow, achieves state-of-the-art performance and effectively handles both object removal and object insertion with flexible mask input.

中文摘要翻译: 3D场景修复对于重建因遮挡或视角受限而损坏的区域至关重要。尽管近期的方法利用高斯泼溅（GS）实现高效的3D编辑，但它们往往依赖精确的多视图分割掩码，并且本质上局限于物体移除任务。我们提出CoIn，一种新颖的框架，通过多阶段一致性流程桥接2D修复模型和3DGS。我们的方法首先利用扩散模型生成初始修复图像，从而能够使用任意形状的掩码并执行如物体插入等多样化任务。接着，我们引入带有特征注意力的参考自适应GS，通过自适应地对参考视图进行加权来重建粗糙的3D场景（2D→3D）。该3D表示通过基于GS的参考特征扭曲为扩散过程提供几何引导，确保多视图一致性（3D→2D）。最后，纹理增强判别器对3D场景进行精炼，以实现高度的光度真实感（2D→3D）。实验表明，CoIn有效利用双向信息流，达到了最先进的性能，并且能够灵活地处理带有任意掩码输入的物体移除和物体插入任务。

</details>


### [22] [Dismantling Pathological Shortcuts: A Causal Framework for Faithful LVLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27596)
*Liu Yu,Can Chen,Ping Kuang,Zhikun Feng,Fan Zhou,Gillian Dobbie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Fox框架，通过检测注意力头在关键决策步骤中脱离视觉证据、锁定语言先验的结构性错位，并以无训练推理时干预的方式切断幻觉捷径路径，在保持语言丰富性的同时显著降低对象幻觉，超越SOTA方法29.1%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法基于注意力强度假设，但本文发现幻觉的深层原因是特定注意力头在决策关键步骤中充当危险中介，从视觉证据解耦并锁定语言先验，形成绕过视觉锚定的病理捷径。需要对此结构性错位进行诊断和干预。

Method: 提出Fox框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 使用视觉注意力熵探针无监督定位危险中介注意力头；(2) 通过数值logit饱和进行靶向因果干预，物理切断捷径路径；(3) 采用冲突门控协同解码策略，平衡干预带来的忠实度与观测到的流畅性。

Result: 在多项实验中，Fox达到SOTA性能，比SID方法提升29.1%，同时保持了语言生成的丰富性。

Conclusion: 对象幻觉的本质原因是注意力头的结构性错位而非简单的注意力强度不足，通过诊断和干预这种结构错位可以有效缓解LVLM中的对象幻觉，且无需额外训练。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) exhibit sophisticated reasoning but remain susceptible to object hallucination. Deviating from the prevailing attention intensity assumption, we reveal a deeper dynamic structural misalignment: hallucination is triggered at decision-critical steps where specific attention heads, acting as risky mediators, decouple from visual evidence to lock onto language priors. This establishes a pathological shortcut that bypasses visual grounding. To dismantle this, we propose Fox (Faithfulness and Observational-flow via eXpression-rectification), a training-free inference-time framework. Fox diagnoses structural misalignment using a visual attention entropy probe to localize risky mediators unsupervisedly. We then execute a targeted causal intervention via numerical logit saturation to physically sever the shortcut path. Finally, a conflict-gated cooperative decoding strategy reconciles interventional faithfulness with observational fluency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Fox achieves SOTA performance, outperforming SID by 29.1% while preserving linguistic richness. Code is available at https://github.com/Cc2021start/Fox.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）展现出复杂的推理能力，但仍易受对象幻觉的影响。与主流的注意力强度假设不同，我们揭示了一种更深层次的动态结构错位：幻觉是在决策关键步骤中触发的，此时特定的注意力头作为危险中介，从视觉证据中解耦并锁定语言先验。这建立了一条绕过视觉锚定的病理捷径。为消除这一问题，我们提出了Fox（通过表达修正实现忠实度与观测流），一个无需训练、在推理时运行的框架。Fox使用视觉注意力熵探针无监督地定位危险中介，诊断结构错位。然后，我们通过数值logit饱和执行靶向因果干预，物理切断捷径路径。最后，一种冲突门控协同解码策略调和了干预带来的忠实度与观测到的流畅性。大量实验表明，Fox达到了SOTA性能，比SID方法提升29.1%，同时保持了语言丰富性。代码已开源：https://github.com/Cc2021start/Fox。

</details>


### [23] [Qwen-Image-2.0-RL Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27608)
*Yixian Xu,Kaiyuan Gao,Yuxiang Chen,Yilei Chen,Zecheng Tang,Zihao Liu,Zikai Zhou,Deqing Li,Hao Meng,Kuan Cao,Jiahao Li,Jie Zhang,Liang Peng,Lihan Jiang,Ningyuan Tang,Shengming Yin,Tianhe Wu,Xiaoyue Chen,Yan Shu,Yanran Zhang,Yi Wang,Yu Wu,Yujia Wu,Zekai Zhang,Zhendong Wang,Xiao Xu,Kun Yan,Chenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Qwen-Image-2.0-RL 提出了一种基于RLHF和在线策略蒸馏的后训练流程，通过任务特定的组合奖励模型、GRPO强化学习框架和在线策略蒸馏，显著提升了文生图和图像编辑的视觉质量与指令跟随能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散模型在视觉质量和指令跟随方面仍有提升空间，现有方法缺乏针对多维度（对齐、美学、人像保真度）的可靠奖励信号，且单一模型难以同时擅长文生图和图像编辑两种任务。

Method: 1) 构建任务特定组合奖励模型：对VLMs进行微调，采用逐点评分范式+链式思维推理，覆盖对齐、美学、人像保真度（T2I）和指令跟随精度、人脸身份保持（编辑任务）；2) 基于GRPO的可扩展RL训练框架：包含混合无分类器引导策略保持预训练知识、组内奖励范围过滤进行提示筛选、每类奖励权重校准；3) 在线策略蒸馏：通过轨迹级速度匹配将多个T2I和编辑任务专用RL策略教师蒸馏到单个学生模型中。

Result: Qwen-Image-Bench总体得分57.84（+2.61），文生图竞技场Elo评分1193（+78），图像编辑竞技场Elo评分1349（+93），在美学质量、提示遵循度和编辑精度上均有一致提升。

Conclusion: 提出的RLHF+在线策略蒸馏后训练流程能有效提升扩散模型在文生图和图像编辑任务上的综合性能，组合奖励模型和混合CFG策略是关键设计要素。

Original Abstract: We present Qwen-Image-2.0-RL, a post-training pipeline that applies reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) and on-policy distillation (OPD) to improve both the visual quality and instruction-following capability of the Qwen-Image-2.0 diffusion model. To provide reliable reward signals, we construct task-specific composite reward models by fine-tuning vision-language models with a pointwise scoring paradigm and chain-of-thought reasoning. For text-to-image generation, the reward models cover alignment, aesthetics, and portrait fidelity dimensions. For image editing tasks, the reward system addresses instruction-following accuracy and face identity preservation. Building on this reward system, we develop a scalable GRPO-based RL training framework, incorporating a hybrid classifier-free guidance (CFG) strategy to preserve pre-trained knowledge, prompt curation via intra-group reward range filtering, and per-category reward weight calibration. To merge the task-specialized RL policies for T2I and editing, we propose on-policy distillation as the final training stage, which consolidates multiple teachers into a single student model through trajectory-level velocity matching. Extensive evaluation shows that Qwen-Image-2.0-RL achieves 57.84 overall score on Qwen-Image-Bench (+2.61 over the base model), Elo ratings of 1193 in text-to-image arena (+78) and 1349 in image edit arena (+93), demonstrating consistent gains in aesthetic quality, prompt adherence, and editing accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了Qwen-Image-2.0-RL，一个应用基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）和在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的后训练流程，旨在提升Qwen-Image-2.0扩散模型的视觉质量和指令跟随能力。为提供可靠的奖励信号，我们通过微调视觉语言模型，采用逐点评分范式和链式思维推理，构建了任务特定的组合奖励模型。对于文生图任务，奖励模型涵盖了对齐度、美学和人像保真度三个维度；对于图像编辑任务，奖励系统则关注指令跟随准确性和人脸身份保持。基于该奖励系统，我们开发了一个可扩展的基于GRPO的强化学习训练框架，引入了混合无分类器引导策略以保留预训练知识、通过组内奖励范围过滤进行提示筛选，以及每类奖励权重校准。为融合针对文生图和编辑任务的任务专用强化学习策略，我们提出将在线策略蒸馏作为最终训练阶段，通过轨迹级速度匹配将多个教师模型整合到单个学生模型中。大量评估表明，Qwen-Image-2.0-RL在Qwen-Image-Bench上取得了57.84的总体得分（比基座模型提升2.61分），在文生图竞技场上Elo评分为1193（提升78分），在图像编辑竞技场上Elo评分为1349（提升93分），在美学质量、提示遵循度和编辑准确性上均展现出一致的提升。

</details>


### [24] [Denoising ICF Images with Multiplicative Uniform Noise: A Self-Supervised Study Based on the Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27635)
*Gyeongha Hwang,Bradley Thomas Wolfe,Naima Naheed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了针对惯性约束聚变（ICF）图像中乘性均匀噪声的Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse自监督去噪框架，证明了其理论等价性，并在实际ICF图像上取得了显著优于BM3D和Noise2Self的去噪性能（PSNR达21.41 dB，SSIM达0.8358）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: ICF图像受到乘性均匀噪声的严重污染，现有的自监督去噪方法（如Noise2Self）和传统滤波方法（如BM3D）在该场景下性能不佳，亟需一种专门针对ICF图像乘性均匀噪声特性的自监督去噪框架。

Method: 提出Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse框架，核心理论贡献是证明了在log域最小化自监督损失等价于在变换域进行有监督学习。实现上设计了两种变体：变体A使用固定高斯噪声加载，变体B使用逐图像JSON均匀噪声加载，以应对ICF图像的特殊挑战。

Result: 变体B取得最佳结果：平均PSNR 21.41 dB、SSIM 0.8358，相比含噪基线（1.95 dB）提升19.46 dB，大幅优于BM3D log-domain（4.47 dB, SSIM 0.5181）和Noise2Self（4.75 dB, SSIM 0.0177）。变体A取得PSNR 21.39 dB、SSIM 0.8436。

Conclusion: Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse框架在ICF图像乘性均匀噪声去噪任务上显著优于现有方法，且训练过程完全自监督，无需干净参考图像（仅用于定量评估）。

Original Abstract: This paper documents the implementation and evaluation of a self-supervised denoising framework on Inertial Confinement Fusion (ICF) images corrupted by Multiplicative Uniform noise: the \emph{Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse} framework. This framework is developed and analysed in this work; the key theoretical result -- that minimising the log-domain self-supervised loss is equivalent to supervised learning in the transformed domain -- is presented with full proof. We document significant implementation challenges arising from the unique characteristics of ICF imagery, describe the fixes applied at each stage, and report final quantitative results. The log-domain approach with per-image JSON Uniform noise loading (Variant~B) achieves the best result: a mean PSNR of $21.41\db$ and SSIM of $0.8358$, a $+19.46\db$ improvement over the noisy input baseline of $1.95\db$, substantially outperforming BM3D log-domain ($4.47\db$, SSIM $0.5181$) and Noise2Self ($4.75\db$, SSIM $0.0177$). Variant~A, using fixed Gaussian noise loading, achieves $21.39\db$ PSNR and SSIM $0.8436$. Of the three evaluated methods, Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse and Noise2Self are entirely self-supervised during training, requiring no clean ground truth data; BM3D is a classical filter-based method requiring no training at all. The clean reference images are used solely for quantitative evaluation of all three methods.

中文摘要翻译: 本文记录了对受乘性均匀噪声污染的惯性约束聚变（ICF）图像实施自监督去噪框架——即对数域Noisier2Inverse（Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse）框架——的过程与评估结果。本文对该框架进行了开发与分析；关键的理论成果——即在log域最小化自监督损失等价于在变换域进行有监督学习——已给出完整证明。我们记录了因ICF图像独特特性而产生的重大实现挑战，描述了各阶段应用的修复方案，并报告了最终量化结果。采用逐图像JSON均匀噪声加载的对数域方法（变体B）取得了最佳结果：平均PSNR为21.41 dB，SSIM为0.8358，相较于含噪输入基线（1.95 dB）提升了19.46 dB，显著优于BM3D对数域方法（4.47 dB, SSIM 0.5181）和Noise2Self方法（4.75 dB, SSIM 0.0177）。采用固定高斯噪声加载的变体A取得了21.39 dB的PSNR和0.8436的SSIM。在三种评估方法中，Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse和Noise2Self在训练过程中完全自监督，无需干净的真实标注数据；BM3D是一种经典的基于滤波器的方法，完全无需训练。干净参考图像仅用于对三种方法进行定量评估。

</details>


### [25] [AI-Generated Image Recognition via Fusion of CNNs and Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27637)
*Xuan-Bach Mai,Hoang-Minh Nguyen-Huu,Quoc-Nghia Nguyen,Hoang-Tung Vu,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于融合策略的鲁棒方法，用于检测AI生成的图像，在CIFAKE数据集上实现了97.32%的准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着合成数据技术的发展，AI生成的图像质量极高，与真实图像难以区分，对数据的可靠性和真实性构成了严重挑战，因此迫切需要有效的检测方法。

Method: 采用融合策略，结合多种检测方法的优势来识别AI生成的图像。

Result: 在CIFAKE数据集上进行了广泛实验，模型达到了97.32%的准确率，能够有效区分AI生成图像与真实图像。

Conclusion: 所提出的融合方法在AI生成图像检测任务上表现优异，有助于在合成数据泛滥的背景下推进数据认证技术的发展。

Original Abstract: Recent advancements in synthetic data technology have opened a new era where images of remarkable quality are generated, blurring the lines between real-life images and those produced by Artificial Intelligence (AI). This evolution poses a significant challenge to ensuring the reliability and authenticity of data, underscoring the need for robust detection methods. In this paper, we present a robust approach aimed at addressing these pressing concerns. Our methodology revolves around leveraging fusion strategies, combining the strengths of multiple detection methods for identifying AI-generated images. Through extensive experimentation on the CIFAKE dataset, our model showcases remarkable performance, achieving an impressive accuracy rate of 97.32%. This accomplishment underscores the efficacy of our approach in accurately distinguishing between AI-generated images and real-life images, thus contributing to the advancement of data authentication techniques amidst the proliferation of synthetic data.

中文摘要翻译: 合成数据技术的最新进展开启了一个新时代，生成的图像具有卓越的质量，模糊了真实图像与人工智能（AI）生成图像之间的界限。这一演变对确保数据的可靠性和真实性提出了重大挑战，凸显了对鲁棒检测方法的迫切需求。在本文中，我们提出了一种旨在解决这些紧迫问题的鲁棒方法。我们的方法论围绕利用融合策略展开，结合多种检测方法的优势来识别AI生成的图像。通过在CIFAKE数据集上进行广泛实验，我们的模型展现了卓越的性能，达到了97.32%的惊人准确率。这一成果证明了我们的方法在准确区分AI生成图像与真实图像方面的有效性，从而在合成数据泛滥的背景下为数据认证技术的进步做出了贡献。

</details>


### [26] [CascadeOcc: Rethinking 3D Occupancy World Models with Cascaded VQ Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27644)
*Kyumin Hwang,Wonhyeok Choi,Jaeyeul Kim,Jihun Park,Daehee Park,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CascadeOcc提出了一种基于级联向量量化（VQ）机制和自回归框架的占据世界模型，通过粗到细的多尺度架构和时间混合器，在仅依赖视觉输入的情况下，在4D占据预测和运动规划任务上取得了优于现有方法的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前占据世界模型过度依赖外部模态（如语言模型）或外部基础模型，未能充分利用占据表示本身固有的结构潜力。本文旨在通过挖掘占据表示的内在层次化结构信息，而非依赖外部辅助信息，来提升复杂3D场景的表示能力。

Method: 1) 将级联向量量化（VQ）机制集成到自回归框架中，增强占据表示的编码能力；2) 遵循粗到细的原则，通过多尺度架构逐步从全局结构精细化到局部细节；3) 引入TimeMixer模块捕获多尺度时间依赖，构建空间和时间上的双重层次化机制。

Result: 在4D占据预测和运动规划基准上，CascadeOcc在仅使用视觉输入的方法中取得了最优性能，验证了优化内在表示可以成为依赖外部基础模型的有效替代方案。

Conclusion: 优化占据表示的内在结构层次是一种有力的替代方案，可以与依赖外部基础模型的方法相抗衡，为自动驾驶中的感知-规划桥接提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: This letter proposes CascadeOcc, a novel occupancy world model that prioritizes intrinsic structural hierarchy over extrinsic auxiliary modalities for autonomous driving. Occupancy world models -- forecasting the future driving environment and planning the driving trajectory -- effectively bridge perception and planning, but current approaches often heavily rely on external modalities or large language models, failing to fully exploit the inherent structural potential of occupancy representations themselves. To enhance representational capacity for complex 3D scenes, we integrate a cascaded Vector Quantized (VQ) mechanism into an autoregressive framework. Following a coarse-to-fine principle, CascadeOcc progressively refines fine-grained details from global structures through a multi-scale architecture. Additionally, we incorporate a TimeMixer to capture multi-scale temporal dependencies, establishing a dual-hierarchy mechanism in both space and time. Experimental results on 4D occupancy forecasting and motion planning benchmarks demonstrate that CascadeOcc achieves superior performance among vision-centric approaches, validating that optimizing inherent representations is a powerful alternative to relying on external foundation models.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了CascadeOcc，一种新颖的占据世界模型，该模型优先挖掘内在结构层次而非依赖外部辅助模态进行自动驾驶。占据世界模型——预测未来驾驶环境并规划驾驶轨迹——有效地桥接了感知与规划，但当前方法通常过度依赖外部模态或大型语言模型，未能充分利用占据表示本身固有的结构潜力。为增强对复杂3D场景的表示能力，我们将级联向量量化（VQ）机制集成到自回归框架中。遵循粗到细原则，CascadeOcc通过多尺度架构从全局结构逐步细化到精细细节。此外，我们引入TimeMixer捕获多尺度时间依赖关系，在空间和时间上建立双重层次化机制。在4D占据预测和运动规划基准上的实验结果表明，CascadeOcc在仅使用视觉输入的方法中达到了最优性能，验证了优化内在表示是依赖外部基础模型的有力替代方案。

</details>


### [27] [VLM-Aware Meta-Optic Front-End Design for Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27646)
*Chanik Kang,Raphaël Pestourie,Haejun Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CODA框架，通过联合优化连续密度超构光学前端与冻结的CLIP视觉模型，直接以分类损失为优化目标替代传统成像质量指标，在受限超构光学成像条件下显著提升零样本识别精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统机器视觉依赖高质量光学系统，但在尺寸、成本和形态受限的应用中难以实现；紧凑型超构光学虽为替代方案，但受物理效率极限限制。现有光学设计以图像级质量（分辨率、像差校正等）为驱动，与下游识别任务目标不一致，亟需一种直接面向识别任务的光学-算法协同设计方法。

Method: 提出CODA协同设计框架，采用可微成像模型进行连续密度超构光学前端优化，通过基于Maxwell仿真的伴随梯度更新，直接优化冻结零样本CLIP分类器的交叉熵损失，无需学习重建、图像信号处理或图像保真度辅助目标函数。

Result: 在ImageNet-100的二维仿真成像基准上，CODA将CLIP ViT-L/14零样本准确率从聚焦基线的53.75±3.57%提升至65.41±3.99%；优化后的光学系统无需重新优化即可迁移至CLIP、SigLIP和DINOv2等多种视觉模型，并在ImageNet-100、CIFAR-100和Food-101数据集上均有效。

Conclusion: 在受限超构光学成像条件下，将光学设计目标与冻结视觉模型的任务目标对齐，而非遵循传统的成像质量准则，能够有效提升下游识别性能。

Original Abstract: Conventional machine-vision pipelines typically rely on high-quality optics that produce clean, human-interpretable images, and optical design has therefore been driven by image-level criteria such as resolution, aberration correction, and pixel fidelity. However, such optics are often impractical for size-, cost-, or form-factor-constrained applications, where compact meta-optics offer an attractive alternative but operate under strict physical efficiency limits. We propose CODA, a co-design framework that optimizes a continuous-density meta-optic front-end for frozen-model recognition using differentiable image formation and adjoint-gradient updates of Maxwell-based simulations. CODA directly optimizes the cross-entropy loss of a fixed zero-shot CLIP classifier without learned reconstruction, image signal processing, or image-fidelity auxiliary objectives. In a two-dimensional simulated imaging benchmark on ImageNet-100, CODA improves CLIP ViT-L/14 zero-shot accuracy from 53.75 $\pm$ 3.57$\%$ with a focal-concentration baseline to 65.41 $\pm$ 3.99$\%$. The optimized optics further transfer without re-optimization across CLIP, SigLIP, and DINOv2 on ImageNet-100, CIFAR-100, and Food-101. These results demonstrate that, under constrained meta-optic imaging, downstream recognition can be improved by aligning optical design with frozen vision-model objectives rather than conventional image-formation criteria.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的机器视觉流水线通常依赖于能够产生清晰、可人工解读图像的高质量光学系统，因此光学设计一直由分辨率、像差校正和像素保真度等图像级指标驱动。然而，在尺寸、成本或形态受限的应用中，这类光学系统往往不切实际；紧凑型超构光学虽提供有吸引力的替代方案，但受严格的物理效率极限约束。本文提出CODA——一种协同设计框架，利用可微成像模型和基于Maxwell仿真的伴随梯度更新，针对冻结模型识别任务优化连续密度超构光学前端。CODA直接优化固定零样本CLIP分类器的交叉熵损失，无需学习重建、图像信号处理或图像保真度辅助目标函数。在ImageNet-100的二维仿真成像基准测试中，CODA将CLIP ViT-L/14的零样本准确率从聚焦基线的53.75±3.57%提升至65.41±3.99%。优化后的光学系统无需重新优化即可迁移至CLIP、SigLIP和DINOv2等多种模型，并在ImageNet-100、CIFAR-100和Food-101数据集上均有效。这些结果表明，在受限超构光学成像条件下，通过将光学设计与冻结视觉模型的目标对齐，而非遵循传统的成像质量准则，可以有效提升下游识别性能。

</details>


### [28] [Temporal-Emerged Prompting for Segment Anything in Multiframe Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27655)
*Yinghui Xing,Donghao Chu,Shizhou Zhang,Di Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TEP-SAM框架，利用目标随时间逐渐从背景中显现的时序线索，通过全局运动模式与局部运动偏差联合建模，引导SAM模型实现低信噪比红外弱小目标的分割与定位。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法即使配备强大的基础模型和帧间关联机制，在单帧中仍难以区分与背景不可分辨的小目标；而目标随时间推移会逐渐从背景中显现，这一时序线索尚未被有效利用。

Method: TEP-SAM框架通过联合建模全局运动模式和局部运动偏差来定位潜在目标，利用运动差异增强目标区域特征，生成时序显现线索以非交互方式提示SAM模型，从而将大规模语义预训练与任务特定的时序建模相结合。

Result: 大量实验证明了该方法在极低信噪比条件和复杂动态背景下的有效性。

Conclusion: TEP-SAM通过显式利用时序显现线索，有效将SAM适配到多帧红外弱小目标检测这一挑战性任务中，尤其在低SNR和复杂动态背景下表现优异。

Original Abstract: Accurately localizing and segmenting small targets in low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) infrared sequences remains a challenging task. Since targets are often indistinguishable from the background in individual frames, existing methods, even when equipped with advanced foundation model and powerful inter-frame association mechanisms, still fail to detect them. Motivated by the observation that targets tend to emerge gradually from the background over time and become distinguishable, we propose Temporal-Emerged Prompting for Segment Anything Model (TEP-SAM), a principled framework designed to explicitly exploit such temporal-emerged cues to modulate and prompt SAM. TEP-SAM operates by jointly modeling global motion patterns and local motion deviations to locate potential targets. It further enhances target region features by leveraging motion discrepancy, thereby generating temporal-emerged cues for SAM and enabling non-interactive segmentation. By bridging large-scale semantic pretraining with task-specific temporal modeling, TEP-SAM effectively adapts SAM to the challenging multiframe infrared small target detection task. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, particularly under severely low-SNR conditions and in complex dynamic background.

中文摘要翻译: 在低信噪比红外序列中准确定位和分割弱小目标仍然是一项具有挑战性的任务。由于目标在单帧中通常与背景难以区分，现有方法即使配备了先进的基础模型和强大的帧间关联机制，仍然无法检测到它们。受目标随时间推移逐渐从背景中显现并变得可区分的现象启发，我们提出时序显现提示分割一切模型（TEP-SAM），这是一个原则性框架，旨在显式利用这种时序显现线索来调制和提示SAM。TEP-SAM通过联合建模全局运动模式和局部运动偏差来定位潜在目标。它进一步利用运动差异增强目标区域特征，从而为SAM生成时序显现线索并实现非交互式分割。通过将大规模语义预训练与任务特定的时序建模相衔接，TEP-SAM有效地将SAM适配到具有挑战性的多帧红外弱小目标检测任务上。大量实验证明了我们方法的有效性，尤其是在极低信噪比条件和复杂动态背景下。

</details>


### [29] [GeoFace: Consistent Multi-View Face Generation with Geometry-Constrained Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27659)
*Yeji Choi,Jinhyeok Choi,Jaewon Min,Minkyung Kwon,Jin Hyeon Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoFace是一个几何约束的多视图扩散框架，通过联合生成多视图RGB图像和3D面部几何（以FLAME UV位置图表示），并利用几何引导的注意力对齐损失，实现从单张输入图生成几何一致的多视角人脸图像。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的多视图扩散模型在每视图层面能生成逼真的图像，但缺乏显式机制来约束不同视图间的共享3D结构，导致跨视角的几何不一致问题。

Method: 提出统一双流框架，联合生成多视图RGB图像和3D人脸几何（FLAME网格导出的标准UV位置图作为视图不变的共享约束）；通过共享注意力层让外观流与几何流交互；引入几何引导的注意力对齐损失，监督外观与几何token之间的交叉注意力，利用3D一致对应关系使外观流正确参考姿态不变的几何线索。

Result: 在RenderMe-360和NeRSemble数据集上，GeoFace在视觉质量和跨视角几何一致性方面均优于现有方法，并能实现更高效的3D重建。

Conclusion: GeoFace通过显式引入几何约束有效解决了多视图人脸生成中的几何不一致问题，为从单张输入生成一致多视图人脸提供了有效框架。

Original Abstract: We present GeoFace, a geometry-constrained multi-view diffusion framework for consistent face generation from a single input. % While recent multi-view diffusion models achieve photorealistic synthesis at the per-view level, they lack an explicit mechanism to enforce a shared 3D structure across views, often leading to inconsistent geometry across viewpoints. To address this, GeoFace proposes a unified dual-stream framework for joint generation of multi-view RGB images and 3D face geometry, where the appearance and geometry streams interact through shared attention layers. To encourage the two streams to mutually constrain each other, we introduce a geometry-guided attention alignment loss that supervises the cross-attention between appearance and geometry tokens with 3D-consistent correspondences, enabling the appearance stream to correctly reference pose-invariant geometric cues for robust alignment across viewpoints. Geometry is represented as a canonical UV position map, derived from a FLAME mesh fitted to multi-view observations, serving as a view-invariant shared constraint across all generated views. Experiments on RenderMe-360 and NeRSemble demonstrate that GeoFace consistently outperforms existing methods in both visual quality and cross-view geometric consistency, facilitating more efficient 3D reconstruction.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了GeoFace，一个几何约束的多视图扩散框架，用于从单张输入生成一致的人脸图像。虽然最近的多视图扩散模型在每视图层面实现了逼真的合成，但它们缺乏显式机制来强制不同视图之间共享3D结构，常常导致跨视角的几何不一致。为解决这一问题，GeoFace提出了一个统一的双流框架，联合生成多视图RGB图像和3D人脸几何，其中外观流与几何流通过共享注意力层进行交互。为了鼓励两个流相互约束，我们引入了几何引导的注意力对齐损失，该损失监督外观token与几何token之间的交叉注意力，利用3D一致的对应关系，使外观流能够正确参考姿态不变的几何线索，从而实现跨视角的鲁棒对齐。几何结构表示为标准UV位置图，来源于拟合多视图观测的FLAME网格，作为所有生成视图的视图不变共享约束。在RenderMe-360和NeRSemble上的实验表明，GeoFace在视觉质量和跨视角几何一致性方面均优于现有方法，有助于实现更高效的3D重建。

</details>


### [30] [MVPruner: Dynamic Token Pruning for Accelerating Multi-view Vision-Language Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27660)
*Nan Yang,Zhanwen Liu,Linfeng Zhang,Shangyu Xie,Yang Wang,Wenzhuo Zhou,Xiangmo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MVPruner，一种两阶段自适应token剪枝方法，用于提升多视图视觉-语言模型在自动驾驶中的推理效率，在保持高精度的同时显著降低计算开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有token剪枝方法采用固定剪枝率和静态重要性度量，忽略了多视图设置下视图间动态重要性差异以及推理过程中信息需求的演化，导致效率低下。

Method: 两阶段自适应token剪枝方法：第一阶段根据各视图的信息多样性分配剪枝预算，并保留跨阶段贡献一致的token以保持语义表征能力；第二阶段通过指令文本引导预算分配和token选择，确保任务对齐。

Result: DriveMM搭载MVPruner在DriveLM基准上实现87.3%的FLOPs降低、4.97倍预填充阶段加速，同时保持98.5%的准确率。

Conclusion: 多视图VLM在深层编码了任务相关的视图先验且具有动态信息需求，MVPruner通过自适应剪枝对齐模型动态需求，在效率与精度之间取得优异平衡。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) improve generalization and interpretability in autonomous driving but suffer from efficiency issues due to long visual token sequences, particularly in standard multi-view settings. Existing token pruning methods employ fixed pruning rate allocation and static importance metrics, ignoring dynamic inter-view importance differences and the evolving information importance during inference. Our analysis reveals that multi-view VLMs inherently encode task-related view priors in deeper layers and exhibit dynamic information requirements. Motivated by these findings, we propose MVPruner, a two-stage adaptive token pruning method that aligns pruning behavior with the model's dynamic information requirements. The first stage allocates pruning budgets based on the information diversity of each view, and retains tokens with consistent contribution across stages, ensuring semantic representational capacity. The second stage allocates budgets and selects tokens guided by instruction text to guarantee task alignment. Experimental results on four benchmarks demonstrate the superior performance of our method. For example, DriveMM equipped with MVPruner achieves 87.3% reduction in FLOPs, 4.97* speedup in prefilling phase while retaining 98.5% accuracy on DriveLM benchmark.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在自动驾驶中提升了泛化能力和可解释性，但由于长视觉token序列（尤其在标准多视图设置下）导致效率问题。现有的token剪枝方法采用固定的剪枝率分配和静态重要性度量，忽略了视图间动态重要性差异以及推理过程中信息需求的演化。我们的分析揭示了多视图VLM在深层固有地编码了任务相关的视图先验，并表现出动态信息需求。受这些发现启发，我们提出MVPruner，一种两阶段自适应token剪枝方法，使剪枝行为与模型的动态信息需求对齐。第一阶段根据各视图的信息多样性分配剪枝预算，并保留跨阶段贡献一致的token，确保语义表征能力。第二阶段通过指令文本引导预算分配和token选择，保证任务对齐。在四个基准上的实验结果表明了我们方法的优越性能。例如，搭载MVPruner的DriveMM在DriveLM基准上实现了87.3%的FLOPs降低、预填充阶段4.97倍加速，同时保持了98.5%的准确率。

</details>


### [31] [Explainable AI for Biodiversity Monitoring and Ecological Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27667)
*Brinnae Bent,Holly R. Houliston,Jiayi Zhou,Günel Aghakishiyeva,David W. Johnston*
